putumuṟiyil iviyēyaṉṟi ivipōlum piṟavum ceyyavēṇṭumeṉpatil yāṉ karuttu vēṟupāṭu uṭiyē ṉallēṉ. āyiṉum nam tamiḻakattil cila pala nūṟṟāṇṭukaṭku muṉ nikaḻntu pōnta camayappaṇikaḷi mīḷavum uyirppittu eḻacceyya vēṇṭumeṉṟu kūṟi viḻikiṉṟēṉ. iṉṟiya niliyil appaṇi mikavum iṉṟiyamiyātatu. ataṉāl namma variṭiyē camayattuṟi paṟṟiya karuttu vēṟṟumi nīṅkumeṉṟu uṟuti kūṟalām.
13. camayappaṇikkuriya iṉṟi cūḻnili
ippōtu nammiṭiyē cātipaṟṟiyum, poruḷnili paṟṟiyum, araciyaṟ karuttup paṟṟiyum pala vēṟu pirivukaḷ uṇṭākiyirukkiṉṟaṉa. cāti vēṟṟumiyum camaya vēṟṟumiyum tāṇṭavamāṭum nammiṭiyē, kalvi vēṟṟumiyum, kaṭci vēṟṟumiyum pukuntu putup putu vēṟṟumikaḷi mikukkiṉṟaṉa. iv vakiyāl piḷavu perukukiṟatēyaṉṟi, oṟṟumi kāṇpatu ari tākiṟatu. paṇṭi nāḷil cāti vēṟṟumiyūṭē camayamē oṟṟumi uṇṭu paṇṇiṟṟu. iṉṟu nām ataṉi maṟantōm.
iṉṟu, nammuṭiya iḷiñarkaḷ camayattuṟiyil karuttu vēṟupaṭukiṉṟaṉar. camayaniliyaṅkaḷil nikaḻum nikaḻccikaḷiṟ kuṟṟaṅ kaṇṭu kūṟukiṉṟaṉar. cila naṭi muṟikaḷi vēṇṭā veṉa maṟukkiṉṟaṉar cila. ariya karuttukkaḷi ārāyccik kaṇkoṇṭu kaṇṭu kēṭ kiṉṟaṉar; avarkaḷik kāṇavē nammavar palar aruvaruk kiṉṟaṉar. palar avar karuttip puṟak kaṇikkiṉṟaṉar. avarkaḷ avvāṟu māṟupaṭuvataṟkum vēṟupaṭu vataṟkum viṉāviṟkum kāraṇam yāteṉak kaṇṭaṟintavar mikac cilarē iruppar. avviḷiñarkku nam kuṭumpaṅkaḷilō, kallūrikaḷilō camaya niliyaṅkaḷilō camayakkalvi tanta tuṇṭā? camaya nūṟ karuttukkaḷiyēṉum nām kaṟpitta tuṇṭā? avarkaḷi vituviṭuvatālō, puṟakkaṇip patālō, aruvaruppa tālō camayattuṟi paṇpaṭṭu viṭumā? camayavaṟivu vantu viṭumā? eṉpaṉa pōṉṟavaṟṟi nām niṉiyātu pōyiṉōm. pirintēkum kūṭṭattuṭ kūṭṭamāka avarkaḷip pirippatutāṉē ceytiruk kiṉṟōm!
nām pirintatu pōtum. iṉi oṉṟupaṭuvōm. nammi oṉṟupaṭuttaṟkuc camayavuṇarvum camayavoḻukkamum peruntuṇi ceyyum. camayavuṇarvu oḻukkaṅkaḷukkuc camayak kalvivēṇṭum iṉṟiya araciyalnili camayak kalvi tarutaṟku oruppaṭātu; araciyal amippu muṟiyiṉi aṟintirukkiṉṟa nām, camayak kalvi vaḻaṅkumāṟu araciyalārikkēṭpatum aṟamaṉṟu; muṟiyu maṉṟu. ‘eṉ kaṭaṉ paṇi ceytu kiṭappatē’ eṉṟa nāvaraciṉ naṉmoḻi uḷḷattiṟpatiya vēṇṭum. nam kaṭaṉ paṇiceytu kiṭappatē eṉa attiru moḻi nam vāypāṭātal vēṇṭum.
14. camayappaṇi viḷakkam
camayappaṇi yeṉpatu yātu? iṉṟu nammiṭiyē camayappaṇi yeṉpatu mikavum kuṟukiya ellikku vantu viṭṭatu. tīkki ceytu kōṭalum, aṉuṭṭāṉa civapūcikaḷ ceyvatum, tiruk kōyilkaḷil nāṭpūcaṉi āṇṭuviḻāc ceyvatum, celvamikka pōtu tirukkōyil tiruppaṇi ceyvatumē camayappaṇikaḷākak karutappaṭuvaṉa vāyiṉa. ivi potuvāka vēṇṭuvaṉa eṉiṉum ciṟappāka uyirkaṭku vēṇṭum paṇikaḷic ceyvatē camayap paṇiyākum. īcaṉukku aṉpu ceyyavēṇṭum eṉpatu camaya nūlkaḷ vaṟpuṟuttum pēraṟamākum. īcaṉukku aṉpu ceyvatāvatu avaṉuṭiya aṭiyāriṭattu aṉpu ceyvatākum. aṭiyārāvār makkaḷuyir aṉivarumāvār. ākavē makkaḷuyirkkuc cāti, niṟa, araciyal, camaya vēṟṟumi karutāmal nalaṉkaḷic ceyvatu aṭiyavarpāl aṉpu celuttuvatākum. epporuḷiyum ākkuvōṉ īcaṉ eṉavum, apporuḷi āḷumukattāl uytipeṟum makkaḷ aṭiyarāvar eṉavum cēkkiḻār perumāṉ, ñāṉacampantar aṟivuṟuttiyatāka eṭutturippatu civarkaḷ aṟintatākum. makkaḷuyirpāl uṇṭākum aṉpu piṉpu ellā vuyirk kaṇṇum cellum, evvuyirkkum aṉpu ceypavarē tammuyirkkum nalam ceypavarāvar. itaṉi “īcaṉukkaṉ pillār, evvuyirkkum aṉpillār evvuyirkkum aṉpillār tamakkum aṉpillār” eṉavum, ittakiya aṉpu ceyyātārip piṇaṅkaḷ eṉṟu ēcuvārāy, “pēcuvatu eṉ aṟivilāp piṇaṅkaḷi” eṉavum avarkaḷi, “nām iṇaṅkiṉ, piṟappilum iṟappiṉilum piṇaṅkiṭuvar” eṉavum aruṇanti civaṉār aṟintu kūṟuvatu niṉivu kūṟattakkatu. ivvāṟu evvuyirkkum aṉpuceytu vāḻvatē “viyattu vāḻvāṅkuvāḻum vāḻvākum; ivvāḻvu ṭiyārē “vāṉuṟiyum teyvamāvar” eṉat tiruvaḷḷu varum, “aṉpar paṇiceyya eṉi āḷākki viṭṭu viṭṭāl, iṉpa nili tāṉē vanteytum” eṉat tāyumāṉārum viḷakkiyaruḷiṉar. ittakiya cittānta vāḻvu nammiṭiyē nilavukiṟatā eṉpati iṟivaṉ tirumuṉ niṉṟu nam neñci nāmē ārāyntu teḷital vēṇṭum.
makkaḷuyirkkum, evvuyirkkum aṉpu ceytal vēṇṭum eṉiṉ, ataṉi eppaṭic ceyvatu eṉṟa viṉā eḻum. uyirkkup paṇiceyvateṉpatu uyirkku uṟuti payakkum kalviyaṟivum, uṭaṟku nalaṉ payakkum uṇavum maruntum utavutal ākum; ituvē aṉpu ceyvār ceyyum uyirp paṇiyākum; itaṉic ceyyum camayamē innāḷil ulakameṅkum paraviyirukkiṟatu.
15. muṉṉāḷil civampurinta camayappaṇi
ippaṇiyi nam civam ceytatuṇṭā? itaṉic ceyvatu camayappaṇiyeṉa nīṅkaḷē kūṟukiṉṟīrkaḷā? eṉṟu kēṭkalām. ikkēḷvi eḻutaṟkuk kāraṇam civa camaya varalāṟu nam nāṭṭil paravāmiyē eṉṟu kūṟalām. neṭuṅkāla ellikkup pōka vēṇṭā. patiṉōrām nūṟṟāṇṭiṉ iṭiyilē cōḻa vēntaruḷ vīrarācēntiraṉ eṉpāṉ, kaṅkikoṇṭa cōḻapuratti liruntu āṭcipurintu varukiyil nāṭṭil maḻiyillāmiyāl pañca muṇṭāyiṟṟu. akkālattē peruntuṉpattuk kuḷḷāṉa varkaḷil tañci mānāṭṭil ālaṅkuṭip pakutiyiṉarāvar. appōtu ālaṅkuṭic civaṉ kōyilār 1011 kaḻañcu poṉṉum, 464 palam veḷḷiyum ūravarkkuk koṭuttu, uṇavukkum vitikkum nel. peṟṟukkoḷḷumāṟu utaviṉar eṉa av vēntaṉuṭiya kalveṭṭoṉṟu kūṟukiṟatu. 1ceṅkaṟpaṭṭu mānāṭṭil vālācāpāttukku aṇmiyil pālāṟṟaṅkariyil tirumukkūṭal eṉṉum ūr oṉṟu uḷatu. avvūril perumāḷ kōyilil caṉanāta maṇṭapameṉa oru maṇṭapamuṇṭu. ataṉ kaṇ, maruttuvac cāliyoṉṟum vētam, carittiram, ilakkaṇam mutaliyaṉa kaṟpikkum kallūriyoṉṟum iruntaṉa. kallūri māṇavarkku uṟiyuḷum, uṇavum, caṉikkiḻamitōṟum eṇ ṇeyum, nāḷum iravil viḷakkerikka eṇṇeyum kōyil tantu utaviṟṟu. maruttuvac cāliyil patiṉintu paṭukkikaḷ iruntaṉa. maruttuvar oruvar iraṇavittiyar oruvar, maruntu ceytu koṭuppōr iruvar, cikiccimakaḷir iruvar, potup paṇi ceyvōṉ oruvaṉ iruntaṉar. avarkku ōrāṇṭukkuriya maruntu vakikaḷuḷ kōmūttira aritaki eṉa aritaki vaki kaḷum, kaṇṭīram, palā kōraṇṭa tiyilam, mutaliya tayila vakikaḷum vilvāti kirutam, maṇṭūkaravaṭakam mutaliya vaccira kalpam, kaliyāṇa lavaṇam īṟākap pala maruntu vakikaḷ aḷikkap paṭṭaṉa eṉa akkōyil kalveṭṭāṉṟu1 kūṟukiṟatu. tiruk kaṇṇa purattukkarukiluḷḷa tiruvirāmaṉatīccuram eṉṉum kōyi lār, taṅkaḷ kōyilukkirunta 50 vēli nilamum nāḷaṭivil kuṟintu pōkavē, “kōyil kāriyam” kuṉṟippōkak kaṇṭu, tillip peruṅkōyil pēravi mākēccurarkku viṇṇappam ceytukoṇṭaṉar. avarkaḷ uṇmitērntu, cōḻamaṇṭalam, pāṇṭi, koṅku maṇṭalam, toṇṭi maṇṭalam, naṭunāṭu eṉṟa tamiḻ maṇṭalattiluḷḷa civaṉ kōyilkaḷuḷ 10 vēlikku mēṟpaṭṭa kōyilkaḷ āṇṭoṉṟukku oru kala ariciyum, maṟṟavi oru tūṇi orupatakkariciyum irāmaṉa tīccarattukkuk koṭukka vēṇṭumeṉap paṇittaṉar. itu tiripuvaṉa cakkara vartti irācarāca cōḻaṉ kālattup patiṉāṟā māṇṭil naṭantatu3 ivvāṟu civaṉ kōyilkaḷ naṭattiya centamiḻc civapperumakkaḷ kalviyum uṇṭiyum maruntum kuruṭar, muṭavar aṉātikaḷāṉa peṇṭir mutaliyōrukku uṟiyuḷ tantu uṭaṟku uṇavum, uyirkkuk kalviyum tantu camayap paṇi purintuḷḷaṉar. ivaṟṟukkup pala nūṟṟukkaṇakkāṉa kalveṭṭukkaḷ cāṉṟu pakarkiṉṟaṉa. ic camayappaṇikaḷi iṉṟu nam camaya niliyaṅkaḷ purikiṉṟaṉavā? illi yeṉpati nām ellōrum aṟivōm. ippaṇikaḷik kūṭiya aḷavil purintavarum kiṟittuva camayattavariṭiyō iculām camayattavariṭiyō camaya vakiyil karuttu vēṟupāṭu koṇṭu piṇaṅkip pirintu niṟpavar uṇṭā? nam camayattavariṭiyē iruppatu pōla avvaḷavu perun tokiyiṉarāka uḷarā? ivaṟṟi nam camaya niliyaṅkaḷ eṇṇip pārkkiṉṟaṉavā? itu kāṟum pōṉatu pōkaṭṭum. iṉiyēṉum ic camayap paṇikaḷip purivataṟku nammavar karut tūṉṟutal vēṇṭum.
16. civattirumuṟikaḷum civa camaya niliyaṅkaḷum
iṟutiyāka oṉṟu kūṟi eṉ muṉṉuriyi muṭikkiṉṟēṉ. nam nāṭṭuc civaṉkōyilkaḷuḷ mikka celvac ciṟappum tirup paṇic ciṟappumuṭiya kōyilkaḷ cilavākum. avi ac ciṟappuṭiyaṉa vāyiruttaṟkuk kāraṇam, camaya kuravarkaḷāṉa tiruñāṉa campantar mutaliyōrāl pāṭap peṟṟatēyākum. ceṭṭināṭṭuc celvamakkaḷāl tiruppaṇi ceyya peṟṟavi aṉittum tirumuṟip pārāṭṭu peṟṟaviyēyākum. curuṅkac collumiṭattu icciṟappum celvamuṭiya tirukkōyil kaṭku acciṟappum celvamum tantavi tirumuṟit tēvārat tirup pāṭṭukaḷē yākum ittirumuṟit tiruppāṭṭukkaḷi nāṭōṟu iṟivaṉ tirumuṉ ōtavēṇṭumeṉa ovvoru tirukōyililum nam muṉṉōrāl nivantaṅkaḷ viṭappaṭṭaṉa. muṉṉaṇiyil ōtappaṭa vēṇṭiya tirumuṟikaḷ ippōtu eṅkē uḷḷaṉa? civaparamporuḷic centamiḻil vaḻipaṭṭa civarkaḷ, iṉṟu anta moḻiyil tam vaḻipāṭic ceykiṉṟaṉarā? ñāṉa campantar mutaliya civa camaya kuravarkaḷ vaḻipaṭṭa tamiḻ, iṉṟu civa camaya niliyaṅkaḷil taṉ ciṟappiḻantu ōr otukkiṭattē otukik kiṭakkiṉṟatu. tillip peruṅkōyilil maṇṇāḷu vēntaṉ vantu maṉaṅkaṉintu kaṇṭuvaṇaṅkip pōṟṟa takka niliyil irunta tirumuṟit tēvāram, centamiḻ civa naṉmakkaḷāl civaṉ kōyilkaḷil puṟakkaṇikkappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa. inta iḻi nili oḻiya vēṇṭum.
iṉṟu araciyalaviyil ārāyap peṟṟuvarum intu aṟaniliyac caṭṭat tiruttam vantatum, ataṉkaṇ, ciṟanta niliyil iruttaṟkuriya tirumuṟikaḷ iṭam peṟāmiyi namcivarkaḷ kāṇavē illi. tiruttam vaḻaṅkiya civa naṉ makkaḷ tirumuṟikaḷ mutaliṭam peṟa vēṇṭum eṉac ceppā toḻiyiṉum eḻuttilkūṭa eḻutiṉārilli “vēta neṟi taḻit tōṅka” vēta pārāyaṇam kuṟippiṭap paṭṭatu pōla, “civat tuṟi viḷaṅka”, nam civa tirumuṟikaḷ kuṟikkappaṭavēṇṭumeṉa namnāṭṭu pattirikikaḷ ellāvaṟṟiṟkum eḻutiṉōm; avaṟṟi avi veḷiyiṭāmalē iruṭṭaṭippuc ceytaṉa. tamiḻ nāṭṭut tamiḻk kōyilkaḷil tamiḻt tirumuṟikaṭku iṭantara vēṇṭu meṉa aṭipaṇintu kiyēnti niṟpatu tamiḻaṉukku māṉakkē ṭallavā eṉa naṇpar palar aṟivuṟuttiṉar, civar palar camaya niliyaṅkaḷip pātukāppavar pōlap pala kūṟak kāṇkiṉ ṟōmē yoḻiyat tamiḻt tirumuṟikaṭkuc civat tirukkōyil kaḷil mutaliṭam taravēṇṭu meṉṟō, camaya vaḻipāṭukaḷum tirumuṟi vaḻaṅkum centamiḻ vaḻiyāka naṭipeṟa vēṇṭu meṉṟō nāṭaṟiya moḻikiṉṟilar.
17. civa cittānta āciriyarkaḷ
vētam pacu eṉṟum, ākamam ataṉ pāl eṉṟum, mutalvar ōtum tamiḻ ataṉ neyyeṉṟum, meykaṇṭār vaḻaṅkiya civañāṉa pōtam anneyyiṉ cuviyeṉṟum civarkaḷ kūṟuvar. itaṉāl, meykaṇṭār vaḻaṅkiya meynnūl, nālva rōtum tamiḻ, pāl maṇakkum ñāṉamātal eṉpatu orutali. nālvarōtun tamiḻi naṭṭāṟṟil eṟintu viṭṭu, meykaṇṭa ñāṉa nūli maṭṭil kippaṟṟi ñāṉattippeṟa muyaṉṟa camaya niliyaṅkaḷum nāṭṭil uṇṭu. anta ñāṉam aṭippaṭi yillāta ōviyakkūṭamāy uruvākātoḻintati nāṭaṟiyum.
iṉi, ñāṉa nūlpāl pērārvamuṭiyār palar nam nāṭṭil kāṇappaṭu kiṉṟaṉar. ñāṉa nūliṉpāl eḻum ārvam uṇmi yāṉāl, atu anta nūli vaḻaṅkiya ñāṉāciriyar pāl ayarā aṉpiṉiyuṇṭupaṇṇum; avar piṟanta vūrkkut tam piṟappil oru muṟiyēṉum pōyvara vēṇṭumeṉṟa viḻivit tōṟṟu vikkum avar piṟantavūr, piṟanta maṉi mutaliyavaṟṟip palarum uṇṭu makiḻattakka ūkkatti uṇṭākkum. mey kaṇṭār piṟanta peṇṇākaṭamō, avar piṟanta maṉiyirunta kaḷappāḷar mēṭō kaṇṭavar cilar. avar irunta tiruveṇṇey nallūril irukkum meykaṇṭār. tirumaṭattik kāṇpavar, civar kaḷākiya nammi oru muṟiyēṉum caliyā toḻiyār. mey kaṇṭār niliyē ituvāyiṉ, avaruṭiya māṇavarum civañāṉa citti vaḻaṅkiyavarumāṉa aruṇanti civaṉāruṭiya tiruttuṟiyūr celpavar accilariṉum cilar eṉpatu tēṟṟam. maṟṟavarip paṟṟik kūṟuvāṉēṉ?
itu kiṭakka, camayakuravarkaḷāṉa ñāṉacampantar piṟanta maṉi orupuṟam irukka, avar ūr peyar pukivaṇṭi niliyattil evvāṟu uḷatu? tiṉṉavelli tirunelvēli yāka, tiruc ciṉṉāppaḷḷi tiruccirāppaḷḷiyāka, tiṉṉaṉūr tiruniṉṟavūrāka, nikappaṭṭām nākippaṭṭiṉamākat tiruntum innāḷilum, ñāṉa campantar piṟanta cīkāḻi ṣiyyāḷi eṉṟē iṉṉum nilavu kiṟatu. avvūril naṭanta civa cittānta camācamum atu tiruttap paṭa vēṇṭumeṉat tīrmāṉam ceytatu. aḵtu iṉṟu vari tiruntāmikkuk kāraṇam civaruḷḷattil civa
vuṇarvu naṉku patiyāmi yittāṉē kāṭṭukiṟatu! iṉṟu nam nāṭṭil ciṟantu viḷaṅkum camayattavaruḷ kiṟittavarum iculāmiyarum taṅkaḷ camaya mutalvar piṟanta vūrkaḷi ettuṇip perumitattōṭu virumpi nōkkukiṉṟaṉar avarkaḷik kaṇṭēṉum civarkaḷākiya nām tirunta vēṇṭāvā? naṭu nāṭṭip pāṭavanta cēkkiḻār perumāṉ, atu, “aṟantarunāvuk karacum, ālālacuntararum piṟantaruḷa” varum perumiyuṭiya teṉa ettuṇip perumitat tōṭu kūṟukiṉṟār! itaṉāl, periyōr piṟappāl uḷatākum perumi kaṇṭu perumitaṅkoḷvatu civarkaṭkup piṟappiyal pākat tāṉē kāṇappaṭukiṟatu? apperumitam iṉṟu maṟintu kiṭappatu ākātu. cīkāḻiyum tiruvāmūrum, tirunāvalūrum tiruvātavūrum centamiḻc civap perumakkaḷ vāḻvil oru muṟiyēṉum kāṇṭal vēṇṭum; atuvaḻiyāka eytum iṉpamum camayappaṟṟum nammic camayattuṟiyil araṇ ceyyum.
18. camayac coṟpoḻivukaḷ paṟṟiya kuṟippu
muṭivāka oṉṟu. camayac cārpāka nikaḻum viḻākkaḷilum, kaḻaka nikaḻccikaḷilum camayac coṟpoḻivu kaḷ nikaḻttap paṭukiṉṟaṉa. coṟpoḻivukkuriya poruḷ kaḷuḷ tiruttoṇṭar varalāṟukaḷum purāṇavaralāṟukaḷum eṭuttuk koḷḷappaṭu kiṉṟaṉa. kaviiravīntiranāt tākūr kūṟiyatu pōla ikkālam kāraṇa kāriya ārāycci puriyuṅ kālamātaliṉ avaṟṟi mikka cemmiyākak kūṟa vēṇṭum. tiruttoṇṭar varalāṟukaḷuḷ oruvar taṉit tiruntu paṭittuc cintittaṟkuriya varalāṟukaḷum, piṟarkku eṭutturittaṟkuriya varalāṟukaḷum uḷḷaṉa. tiruk kaḷiṟṟup paṭiyār vakutturikkum valviṉi varalāṟukaḷum melviṉi varalāṟukaḷum pakuttaṟintu melviṉi varalāṟu kaḷiyē coṟpoḻivuk kuriyavākak kōṭal ciṟappu. in niliyil, ñāṉacampantar, nāvaracar, nampiyārūrar, kārik kālammiyār, cēramāṉ perumāḷ eṉṟa inta aivar varalāṟu kaḷiyum avarkaḷ vaḻaṅkiya tiruppāṭṭukkaḷukkuc ciṟappaḷittup poruḷ viḷakkam ceytum coṟpoḻivu ceyvatu ciṟanta neṟiyākum. purāṇavaralāṟukaḷuḷ kaṭavuḷari makkaḷiṉum kīḻāṉavarākak kāṭṭum varalāṟu kaḷip paṭakkāṭcikaḷilum nāṭaka mēṭikaḷilum kāṭṭā toḻiyumāṟu civarkaḷākiya nām ēṟpāṭu ceytal vēṇṭum. avi vēṇṭappaṭṭa kālam vēṟu, ikkāla nilivēṟu eṉpati naṉku niṉivu kūra vēṇṭum.
19. muṭippuri
perumakkaḷē, eṉatu talimiyuri mika nīṇṭuviṭṭa teṉpati naṉku uṇarukiṟēṉ. uṅkaḷ poṟumiyiṉi mikavum alittuviṭṭēṉ. āṉāl, icciva cittānta capiyiṉ tōṟṟattukku aminta cūḻ niliyiṉiyum, itu ceyta tiruppaṇiyiṉiyum ataṉāl viḷinta payaṉiyum mutaṟkaṇ kūṟa vēṇṭiya kaṭami vantatu. piṉṉarcciva cittāntameṉpatiṉ poruḷi viḷakkiṉēṉ. ataṉpiṉ, ikkālattil viññāṉa vaḷarcciyiṉ payaṉāy makkaḷ uḷḷam eytiyirukkum nili yiṉiyum, anniliyil tattuva ñāṉam irukkum iruppiṉiyum, ataṉ muṭivil camaya ñāṉam viḷaṅkum cālpiṉiyum kāṭṭi, avi pacu pāca pati ñāṉaṅkaḷāyp paṭimuṟiyil amin tirukkum amippiṉiyum, mēṉāṭukaḷil avi eytiyirukkum iyalpiṉiyum, eṭuttuk kūṟa vēṇṭiyavaṉāyiṉēṉ. piṉpu civa cittāntam, ēṉiya nam nāṭṭuc cittāntaṅkaḷōṭu ottum, uyarntum irukkum nalattik kūṟiṉēṉ. itaṉ kaṇ cilar kūṟum taṭikaṭku ōraḷavu viṭiyaḷittēṉ. ic civa cittāntak karuttukkaḷi nam nāṭṭiṟ parapputaṟku vēṇṭum muṟiyiṉik kūṟumāṟṟāl, camayap paṇiyirukka vēṇṭiya muṟiyiṉiyum, iṭikkālac cōḻapāṇṭiyar kālattil camaya aṟa niliyaṅkaḷ purinta camayap paṇiyiṉik kalveṭṭukkaḷiṉ tuṇi koṇṭu eṭuttōti attakiya paṇikaḷi mīḷavum nammuṭiya niliyaṅkaḷ puriya vēṇṭumeṉa vēṇṭiṉēṉ. piṉpu civaṉ kōyilkaḷil tirumuṟikaḷ irukkum niliyiṉiyum, itaṟkiṭiyē ivaṟṟiyum ivaṟṟit toṭarntu pōnta cittānta nūlkaḷiyum āciriyarkaḷ pāl civarkaḷākiya nām kāṭṭum aṉpiṉmiyiṉiyum, kāṭṭa vēṇṭiya aṉpu neṟiyiṉiyum, camayac coṟpoḻivu ceyyu miṭattu nām kuṟikkoḷḷa vēṇṭiya karuttukkaḷiyum ōraḷavu kāṭṭiṉēṉ. ivvaḷavum amitiyōṭu kēṭṭuc ciṟappitta uṅkaḷ peruntakimikku eṉ vaṇakkattit terivittu eṉṉi inniliyil vittuc ciṟappikkum ic capit talivarkaḷ, ceyalāḷarkaḷ aṉivarukkum eṉ vaṇakkatti mikka paṇi vōṭu terivittuk koṇṭu, eṉṉiyum uṅkaḷiyum ellō riyum oṉṟu kūṭṭi nam civacittāntap perumporuḷi niṉintu. iṉpuṟu vikkum civakuru nātaṉākiya
“teṉtamiḻkkali terintavar poruntiya tēvūr
  aṉpaṉ cēvaṭi aṭintaṉam allal oṉṟilamē”
- tiruñāṉacampantar.
eṉṟu kūṟi muṭikkiṟēṉ. naṉṟi vaṇakkam.


12. civañāṉapōtac cemporuḷ
centilāṇṭavaṉ tuṇi tiruveṇṇeynallūr
meykaṇṭa tēvar aruḷicceyta
civañāṉa pōtac cūttiramum
āṅkila moḻi peyarppum*
kāppu
	kallāl niḻalmali, villār aruḷi
	pollār iṇimalar, nallār puṉivarē.
The Good will crown their heads with the two Feet of Ganesha who was begotten by the Great Teacher, who sat under the Sacred Mountin Banyan tree and removed the doubts of the Great Nandi. 
nūl
mutaṟ cūttiram
pati uṇmikkup piramāṇam
avaṉ avaḷ atueṉum avimū viṉimiyiṉ
tōṟṟiya titiyē oṭuṅkimalat tuḷatām
antam āti eṉmaṉār pulavar.
As the (seen) universe, spoken of as he she and it. undergoes three changes (origin, development and decay), this must be an entity created (by an efficient cuse). This entity owing to its conjunction with Anava Mala has to emanate from Hara to whom it returns during Samharam. Hence, the learned say that Hara is the First Cause.
iraṇṭāñ cūttiram
pāca uṇmikkup piramāṇam
	aviyē tāṉē yāyiru viṉiyiṉ
	pōkku varavu puriya āṇiyiṉ
	nīkkam iṉṟi niṟkum aṉṟē.
He is one with the souls (Abetha). he is different from them (Betha). He is one and different from them (betha betha). He stands in Samava union with His Gnana Sakti and cuses the souls to undergo the processes of evolution (births) and return (Samharam) by including their good and bad acts (Karma).
mūṉṟāñ cūttiram
pacu uṇmikkup piramāṇam
	uḷatuilatu eṉṟaliṉ eṉatuṭal eṉṟaliṉ
	aimpulaṉ oṭukkam aṟitaliṉ kaṇpaṭil
	uṇṭiviṉi iṉmiyiṉ uṇartta uṇartaliṉ
	māyā iyantira taṉuviṉuḷ āṉmā.
It rejects every portion of the body as not being itself; it says my body; it is conscious of dreams; it exists in sleep without feeling pleasure or pin or movements; it knows from others; this is the soul which exists in the body formed as a machine from Maya.
nāṉkāñ cūttiram
pacuviṉ potu ilakkaṇam
	antak karaṇam avaṟṟiṉoṉ ṟaṉṟuavi
	cantittatu āṉmāc cakacamalat tuṇarātu
	amiccaracu ēyppaniṉṟu añcavat tittē.
The soul is not one of the Andakaranas It is not conscious when it is in conjunction with Anavamala. It becomes conscious only when it meets the Andakarana, just as a king understands through his ministers. The relation of the soul to the fvie Avastha is also similar.
aintāñ cūttiram
pāca ilakkaṇam
	viḷampiya uḷḷattu meyvāy kaṇmūkku
	aḷantaṟintu aṟiyā āṅkavi pōlat
	tāmtam uṇarviṉ tamiyaruḷ
	kāntaṅ kaṇṭa pacācat taviyē.
The senses while perceiving the object cannot perceive themselves or the soul; and they are perceived by soul. Similarly, the soul while perceiving cannot perceive itself (while thinking cannot think thought) and God. It is moved by the Arul Sakti of God, as the magnet moves the iron, while Himself remins immoveable or unchangeable.
āṟāñ cūttiram
pati ilakkaṇam
	uṇaruru acatteṉiṉ uṇarātu iṉmiyiṉ
	irutiṟaṉ allatu civacat tāmeṉa
	iraṇṭu vakiyiṉ icikkumaṉ ulakē.
That which is perceived by the senses is Asat (changeable). That which is not so perceived does not exist. God is neither the one nor the other, and hence called Siva Sat or Chit Sat by the wise; Chit or Siva when not understood by the human intelligence and Sat when perceived with divine wisdom.
ēḻāñ cūttiram
pacuviṉ ciṟappu ilakkaṇam
		yāviyum cūṉiyam cattetir ākaliṉ
		cattē aṟiyātu acattilatu aṟiyātu
		irutiṟaṉ aṟivuḷatu iraṇṭalā āṉmā.
In the presence of  Sat, every thing elese (cosmos-Asat) is Sunyam (non-apparent). Hence Sat cannot perceive Asat. As Asat does not exist, it cannot perceive Sat. That which perceives both, cannot be either of them. This is the soul (called Satasat).
eṭṭāñ cūttiram
ñāṉam uṇarum muṟi
	aimpula vēṭariṉ ayarntaṉi vaḷarnteṉat
	tammutal kuruvumāyt tavattiṉil uṇarttaviṭṭu
	aṉṉiyam iṉmiyiṉ araṉkaḻal celumē.
The Lord appearing as Guru to the soul which had advanced in Tapas (virtue and knowledge) instructs him that he has wasted himself by living among the savages of the five senses; and on this, the soul, understanding its real nature, leaves its former associates and being different from Him, becomes united to His Feet.
oṉpatāñ cūttiram
āṉma cutti
	ūṉakkaṇ pācam uṇarāp patiyi
	ñāṉak kaṇṇiṉiṟ cinti nāṭi
	urāttuṇit tērtteṉap pācam oruvat
	taṇṇiḻa lāmpativiti eṇṇumañ ceḻuttē.
The soul, on perceiving in itself with the eye of Gnanam, the Lord who cannot be perceived by the human intellect or senses, and on giving up the world (Pasa) by knowing it to be false as a mirage, will find its rest in the Lord. Let the soul contemplate Sri Panchatchara according to Law.
pattāñ cūttiram
pāca nīkkam
	avaṉē tāṉē ākiya anneṟi
	ēka ṉāki iṟipaṇi niṟka
	malamāyi taṉṉoṭu valviṉi iṉṟē.
As the Lord becomes one with the soul in its human condition, so let the soul become one with Him and perceive all its actions to be His. Then will it lose all its Mala, Maya and Karma.
patiṉōrāñ cūttiram
civappēṟu
	kāṇuṅ kaṇṇukkuk kāṭṭum uḷampōl,
	kāṇuḷ ḷattik kaṇṭu kāṭṭaliṉ
	ayarā aṉpiṉ araṉkaḻal celumē.
As the Soul enables the eye to see and itself sees, so Hara enables the soul to know and itself knows. And this Adwaitha knowledge and undying Love will unite it to His Feet.
paṉṉiraṇṭāñ cūttiram
cīvaṉ muttariṉ taṉmi
	cemmalar nōṉṟāḷ cēral oṭṭā
	ammalaṅ kaḻīi aṉparoṭu marīi
	mālaṟa nēyam malintavar vēṭamum
	ālayan tāṉum araṉeṉat toḻumē.
Let the Jivathma after washing off its Mala which separates it from the strong Lotus Feet of the Lord and mixing in the society of Bhaktas (Jivan Muktas) whose souls abound with love, having lost dark ignorance, contemplate their Forms and the Forms in the temples as His Form.
civañāṉa pōtam
potu atikāram	uṇmi atikāram
	piramāṇa	ilakkaṇa	cātaṉa	payaṉ
	iyal	iyal	iyal	iyal
	(1,2,3 cūt.)	(4,5,6 cūt.)	(7,8,9 cūt)	(10,11,12 cūt.)
			
civañāṉa pōta mūlamum
āṅkila moḻi peyarppum
muṟṟiṟṟu.



civañāṉa pōtac cemporuḷ
kāppu
	kallāl niḻalmali, villār aruḷiya
	pollār iṇimalar, nallār puṉivarē.
nam kaṇṇukkup pulaṉākum ivvulakam, uyiruṭip poruḷ uyiril poruḷ eṉavum, iyaṟkip poruḷ ceyaṟkip poruḷ eṉavum, parup poruḷ nīriyaṟ poruḷ kāliyaṟ poruḷ eṉavum, ceyyappaṭum ārāyccik kēṟpa vakuttu ārāyappaṭum. camayattuṟiyil niṉṟu ārāycci ceyvōr taṅkaḷ taṅkaḷ camayak koḷkikaṭ kēṟpap poruḷkaḷip pakuttu ārāyvar. tamiḻilakkaṇa nūlōr poruḷkaḷi makkaṭ poruḷ eṉṟum piṟa poruḷ eṉṟum pakuttu nōkkuvar. ivvulakiṭi niṉṟu aṟivārāycci ceyyum aṟiñar aṉivarum tamakkēṟṟa vakiyil ivvulakam pakuttu nōkkappaṭum pāṉmiyuṭiyatāyiruppatik kāṇkiṉṟaṉar. tamiḻ nūṉ muṟip paṭi ivvulakam, uyartiṇi aḵṟiṇi eṉa iruvakiyāka vittup poru ḷārāycci ceyyumāṟu amintuḷatu. uyartiṇip poruḷ āṇ eṉṟum, peṇ eṉṟum pakukkappaṭukiṟatu; ēṉi aḵṟiṇip poruḷiṉkaṇ uyiruṭiyaṉa cila āṇ peṇṇeṉap pakuttuṇarap paṭiṉum uyiruṭip poruḷuṭ cilavum, uyiril poruḷ aṉittum āṇ peṇ pākupāṭṭiṟku akappaṭuvatilli; ataṉāl apporuḷ atu eṉṟa collālum, uyartiṇi āṇ avaṉ eṉṟacollālum, uyartiṇip peṇ avaḷ eṉṟa collālum kuṟikkappaṭuvatu tamiḻ paṭippavar aṉivarum aṟintatu. 
itaṉāl ivvulaku muṟṟum avaṉ, avaḷ, atu eṉṟu kuṟikkappaṭum poruḷkaḷiṉ tokutiyē yeṉpatu teḷivām. āṉatu paṟṟiyē meykaṇṭa tēvar ivvulakatti, "avaṉ avaḷ atu eṉum avi" eṉṟu kuṟittaruḷiṉār. āṇum peṇṇumāyp palar kūṭiyirukkum iṭam avi yeṉappaṭum. avvāṟē, avaṉ, avaḷ, atu eṉṉum mūvakip poruḷum kūṭi iyaṉṟuḷḷa ulakum avi yeṉṟu kuṟikkappaṭṭatu.
nām kāṇum ovvōr aviyum makkaḷ vantu kūṭuṅkāl tōṟṟamum, irukkuṅkāl nili pēṟum, pirintu pōkuṅkāl kēṭum eytukiṟatu. itupōlavē ivvulakamākiya aviyum tōṟṟam, nili, iṟuti yeṉṟa mūṉṟu ceyalkaḷiyum uṭiya teṉpatu iṉitu viḷaṅkukiṟatu. nām kāṇum avikaḷ tāmē kūṭuvatiṉṟi, oruvar muṉṉiṉṟu kūṭṭak kūṭalum, irutta iruttalum, pirikkap piritalum uṭiyatu; itupōlavē iv vulakamum tāṉē tōṉṟātu mutalvaṉ oruvaṉ tōṟṟuvikkat tōṉṟalum, niṟpikka nilipeṟutalum, oṭukka oṭuṅkutalum uṭiyateṉpatu tāṉē viḷaṅkum. nammāṟ kāṇappaṭum ulakam, oruvar kūṭṭak kūṭiyirukkum avi pōlvatākiyāl, oruvar tōṟṟuvikkat tōṉṟi nilipeṟukiṟatu eṉpatu teḷivām. ātalāl, āciriyar, avaṉum avaḷum atuvumākiya poruḷkaḷ kūṭiyirukkum ivvulaka mākiya avikkut tōṉṟutal mutaliya mūviṉikaḷ1 irukkiṟapaṭiyāl, oruvar kūṭṭak kūṭiyirukkum avipōla, ulakam tōṟṟuvikkat tōṉṟi nili peṟukiṟatu eṉṟu kūṟukiṉṟār. itaṉi mutaṟcūttiram, avi mūviṉi uṭimiyiṉ tōṟṟiya titiyē eṉṟu kūṟukiṟatu. itu, tāṉē tōṉṟiya taṉṟu eṉpataṟkākat tōṉṟiya titi eṉṟu collāmal tōṟṟiya titi eṉṟār. ataṟku, tāṉē tōṉṟāmal tōṟṟu vikkappaṭṭu uḷatāyatu eṉṟu poruḷ kūṟappaṭum.
ulakil nām kūṭṭak kūṭum avi muṭintavaḻi, avva viyiṟ kūṭiyiruntōr tattam maṉikkuc ceṉṟu oṭuṅkuvar; mīḷa nām kūṭṭumpōtu  avarkaḷ tām oṭuṅkiyirunta maṉiyiliruntu vantu kūṭuvar. ivvaṇṇamē ivvulakamākiya aviyum oṭuṅkum pōtu iṟivaṉiṭattilē ceṉṟoṭuṅki, mīḷat tōṟṟuvik kumpōtu avaṉiṭattiruntē vantu ulakamākiya aviyāyk kāṭciyaḷikkiṟatu. ulakatti oṭukkum pōtu, aḵtu oṭuṅkiyiruppataṟkut tāṉē iṭamātalāl, iṟivaṉi "oṭuṅki" eṉṟu āciriyar kūṟukiṉṟār. oṭuṅkiya ulakam tāṉ oṭuṅkiyiruppataṟku iṭamāṉa oṭuṅkiyiliruntē tōṉṟuvatu koṇṭu. "oṭuṅki uḷatām " eṉṟār. eṉavē, avaṉ avaḷ atu eṉpavaṟṟiṉ tokutiyākiya avi tōṟṟiya titiyāyk kāṭciyaḷittu oṭuṅkum eṉṟum, tāṉ oṭuṅkiyirunta oṭuṅki yiṉiṉṟē mīḷa uḷatām eṉṟum terivitta vāṟāyiṟṟu.
orunāḷ kūṭiya oru pēravi maṟunāḷum mīḷakkūṭumāyiṉ, nām eṉṉa niṉippōm? muṉṉāḷ kūṭiyataṉ nōkkam kikūṭāmiyāl maṟunāḷum kūṭi yirukkiṟa teṉṟum, kikūṭāmikkuk kāraṇamāyirunta taṭi muṉṉāḷē nīṅkātu maṟunāḷ kūṭuvataṟkuk kāraṇamāyiṟ ṟeṉṟum tāṉē niṉippōm. atu pōlavē, orukāl tōṟṟuvikkappaṭṭa ulakam, tōṉṟiyatukoṇṭu peṟṟuk koḷḷa vēṇṭiya payaṉip peṟṟuk koḷḷāmiyāl oṭuṅki mīḷat tōṟṟuvikkappaṭutāyiṟṟeṉṟum, peṟṟuk koḷḷāvāṟu taṭuttirunta taṭi yiṉāl mīḷat tōṟṟuvikkap paṭukiṟa teṉṟum eṇṇavēṇṭum. attaṭiyātu eṉa ārāyntu aḵtāvatu malam eṉṟu kaṇṭataṉāl, ulakamākiya avi oṭuṅki malattāl mīḷa avvoṭuṅ kiyiṉiṉṟē uḷatām eṉṟu āciriyar aṟivuṟuttukiṉṟār.
ivvāṟu oṭukkuvatum oṭuṅkiyataṟku iṭamum ātalāl iṟivaṉ ulakukku antamum, mīḷat tōṟṟuvittaṟkum avaṉē kāraṇamātalāl ātiyumākiṉṟāṉ. taṉakku ātiyum antamum illāta iṟivaṉ, ulakiṟku antamum ātiyumāvatu paṟṟi, "antam āti eṉmaṉār pulavar" eṉṟu āciriyar kūṟukiṉṟār. eṉmaṉār pulavar eṉpataṟku, eṉṟu colluvar ārāycciyuṇarvu paṭitta aṟiñar eṉṟu poruḷ koḷḷa vēṇṭum.
iccūttirattāl cila karuttukkaḷ teḷivupaṭa vēṇṭum. ōr avi kūṭṭap peṟutaṟku oru nōkkam iruppatu pōla, ulakap paṭippukkum oru nōkkam iruttal vēṇṭum. annōkkam yātu? annōkkam kikūṭātu taṭukkum malamyātu? ulakap paṭippu annōkkatti evvāṟu kikūṭṭuvikkum?
ivvulakam uyirum uyiril poruḷumākak kāṭci aḷikkiṟatu. ivvāṟu kāṭciyaḷikkac ceyvatutāṉ ulakap paṭippu eṉpatu. uyirkaḷ ākkappaṭuvatu milli; aḻivatumilli. iṟivaṉ eṉṟu uṇṭō aṉṟē uyirkaḷ uṇṭu. iṟivaṉ oruvaṉ; uyirkaḷ eṇṇiṟantaṉa. cempiṉ kaṇ kaḷimpu uṭaṉiruppatu pōla uyirkaḷiṭattē malam uṭaṉ irukkiṟatu. kaḷimpu nīṅkiṉa cempu oḷiviṭṭut tikaḻvatupōla, malam nīṅkiṉa uyir ñāṉavoḷi ciṟantu pēriṉpam eytum. cempiṉkaṇ kaḷimpu eppoḻutu ēṉ uṇṭāyiṟ ṟeṉṟa kēḷvikku viṭi kiṭiyātu; atu pōla uyir kaṭku malam eppoḻutu ēṉ uṇṭāyiṟ ṟeṉṟu kāṇpatu ākāta oṉṟu. itaṉi aṉāti campantam eṉṟu aṟivunūlōr kūṟuvar. uyiriṉ vēṟākak kāṭciyaḷikkum uṭalum karuvikaḷum nukar poruḷumākiya ulakam ivvāṟu kāṇappaṭumāṟu ceyvatutāṉ paṭippu eṉpatu. ivvulakattukku mutaṟkāraṇam oṉṟu uṇṭu. uyiril poruḷākiya ivvulakam oṭuṅkuṅkāl mutalil nuṇporuḷāy oṭuṅkum; atuvē piṉṉar āṟṟalāy (Energy) oṭuṅkum; anta āṟṟal (cakti) māyi yeṉṟu nūlkaḷiṟ kūṟappaṭum. iṟivaṉ oḷiyum oḷip poruḷum pōlvaṉ. oḷipporuḷiṉiṉṟum nīṅkāmal irukkum oḷipōla avaṉiṭat tiṉiṉṟum nīṅkāmal irup patu avaṉatu aruḷ. aḵtu aruṭcatti yeṉṟum vaḻaṅkum. āṟṟalāy oṭuṅkiya māyi avaṉatu aruṭcattiyil taṅkum, anta aruṭcatti iṟivaṉākiya civattil oṭuṅkiyirukkum oṭuṅyākiya civaṉ, māyiyi mīḷat tōṟṟuvikkak karutuvāṉāyiṉ, aruṭcattiyi nōkkuvaṉ; atu māyiyākiya caktiyi veḷippaṭutti, uṭampum karuviyum poruḷum ulakumāka virintu kāṭcip paṭac ceyyum. ataṉi itupaṟṟiyē, “oṭuṅkiyuḷatām” eṉṟār.
mutaṟ kāraṇamākak koṇṭu uyirkaṭkariya uṭampoṭu kūṭi vāḻum uyir malamaṟippu nīṅki aṟivu viḷaṅkap peṟukiṟatu. uyirkku anta naṉṉili eyta vēṇṭumeṉṟa pēraruḷāl ivvulakam mīḷap paṭikkappaṭukiṟatu. aṟivuruvāka irukkum uyirōṭu koṇṭa aṉāti campantattāl, malam ataṉ aṟivi maṟittu, ulakiṭit tōṉṟip peṟutaṟkuriya aṟivu viḷakkatti nirampap peṟātavāṟu taṭi ceykiṉṟatu. aṟivu viḷakkam peṟṟu ataṉ vaḻiyāka eytum pēriṉpatti nukarvatutāṉ uyiriṉ nōkkam; ataṟkut tuṇipurivatu, uṭal karuvi mutaliyavaṟṟiṟku mutaṟ kāraṇamākiya māyi. ik karuttukkaḷ viḷaṅkavē, āciriyar meykaṇṭār,
“	avaṉ avaḷ atueṉum avimū viṉimiyiṉ
 	tōṟṟiya titiyē oṭuṅkimalat tuḷatām
	antam āti eṉmaṉār pulavar”
eṉṟu aṟivuṟut taruḷukiṉṟār.
2. avaṉ avaḷ atu eṉṉum mūvakip poruḷum kūṭik kāṭciyaḷikkum ulakamākiya aviyit tōṟṟuvittu nili peṟuvittaṟku iṟivaṉ taṉatu aruṭcattiyi nōkkuvaṉ eṉṟu muṉṉē kaṇṭōm. anta aruṭcatti iṟivaṉākiya mutalvaṉukku āṇi eṉṟu collappaṭum. avaṉ niṉippiyē taṉakku āṇiyākak koṇṭu avvaruṭcatti iyaṅkuvatāl aḵtu āṇi eṉṟu kūṟappaṭukiṟatu. anta āṇiyum iṟivaṉum, kuṇamum kuṇiyum pōlap pirintu nillāta taṟkiḻamit toṭarpu uṭiyavi; centāmarip pūviṉ cemmi pirikkamuṭiyātu oṉṟiyiruppatu pōla iṟivaṉatu āṇiyākiya aruṭ catti avaṉiṟ piriyatu oṉṟi niṟkum. itupaṟṟiyē āciriyar, antam ātiyeṉa mutaṟ cūttirattiṟ kūṟappaṭṭa iṟivaṉākiya mutalvaṉ “āṇiyiṉ nīkkamiṉṟi niṟkum” eṉṟa aṟivuṟuttukiṉṟār, nīṅki niṟpataṟkum nīkkamiṉṟi niṟpataṟkum urimiyuṭiyavaṉ mutal vaṉātalāl, āṇi nīkkamiṉṟi niṟkum eṉṟu collāmal, mutalvaṉ “āṇiyiṉiṉṟum nīkkam iṉṟi niṟkum ” eṉṟu colliyaruḷukiṉṟār.
iṉi, ivvulakattukku antamum ātiyumāy niṟkum iṟivaṉ irukkum iyalpu kūṟaluṟṟa āciriyar, ulakamākiya aviyēyāy, tāṉēyāy, aviyē tāṉēyāy niṟkiṉṟāṉ eṉṟu kūṟukiṉṟār. mutalvaṉ tāṉēyāy, aviyēyāy niṟkum, avi yāyum tāṉāyum uṭaṉ niṟkum eṉpatu karuttu. mutalvaṉ tāṉēyāy niṟpateṉpatu, mutalvaṉ ulakuyirkaḷōṭu kalakkāmal vēṟāyt taṉittu niṟkum nili; aviyēyātaleṉpatu, ulakamākiya avi vēṟu tāṉ vēṟu eṉṉātavāṟu oṉṟāyk kalantu niṟkum nili; uyirum uṭampumāyk kalantu nilavum nampāl, uyir vēṟu uṭampu vēṟu eṉap pirittuk kāṇātapaṭi nammuyir uṭampōṭu kalantu oṉṟāy niṟpatu pōla, mutalvaṉ ulakil oṉṟāyk kalantu niṟkiṉṟāṉ eṉpatu. ulakil oṉṟāyk kalantu niṟkuṅkāl ulakam mutalvaṉātalum, mutalvaṉ ulakamātalum illi. evvāṟeṉṟāl, uṭampōṭu oṉṟāyk kalantu niṟkum uyir uṭampāvatum, uṭampu uyirāvatum illi yaṉṟō, atupōla eṉa aṟital vēṇṭum. ulakil nilavum uyirkaṭku, aṟivu viḻivu ceyal eṉṟa mūṉṟu iyalpukaḷ uṇṭu. aṟivu mutaliya iyalpukaḷ veḷippaṭutaṟkuriya karuviyoṭu kūṭumāyiṉ, uyir, aṟivatō viḻivatō ceyvatō mēṟkoḷḷum. uyiriṉ vēṟāya ulakiyaṟ poruḷkaṭku attakiya ceyal kiṭiyātu. apporuḷkaḷ uyirpporuṭṭuc ceyaṟpaṭavēṇṭi yiruppatāl mutalvaṉ avaṟṟōṭu oṉṟāyk kalantu niṟkiṉṟāṉ. avvāṟu nillāṉāyiṉ, ulakiyaṟ poruḷ oru neṟippaṭa oḻukutaṟku ētu illiyākum; ikkāla viññāṉa vaḷarc cikkum iṭamilliyām.
ulakiyaṟ poruḷāl ākiya uṭampiṉkaṇ niṟkum uyir malat taṭiyiliruntu nīṅka vēṇṭumeṉṟu, ulaka vāḻkkiyit toṭaṅkum; vāḻkkiyil, aṟivaṉavaṟṟi aṟiyavum, viḻiyavum, ceyyavum iṟivaṉ uṭaṉiruntu tuṇi ceykiṉṟāṉ. uṭampōṭu oṉṟāyum, uyiriṉ vēṟāyum, iraṇṭiṉiyum viṭṭu nīṅkāmalum, iruppatāl avvāṟu irukkum mutalvaṉatu iruppu uṭaṉātal eṉppaṭukiṟatu; iraṇṭām cūttiram, aviyē tāṉē yāy eṉṟu uṇarttukiṟatu. ulakiṟku antam ceyyum ātiyākiya mutalvaṉ antamum ātiyumākumpōtu vēṟāy niṟpatum, uyiril poruḷākiya ulakiyaṟ poruḷkaḷōṭu oṉṟāy niṟpatum, uyirkaḷōṭu uṭaṉāy niṟpatum uṇartta vanta tiruñāṉa campantap perumāṉ,
“īṟāymutal oṉṟāyiru peṇṇāṇkuṇa mūṉṟāy
 māṟāmaṟi nāṉkāyvaru pūtammavi aintāy
 āṟārcuvi ēḻōciyo ṭeṭṭuttici tāṉāy
 vēṟāyuṭa ṉāṉāṉ iṭam vīḻimmiḻa liyē*”
eṉṟu aruḷic ceytuḷḷār. vēṟāy niṉṟu antamum ātiyumām tiṟatti, “vēṟāy īṟāy mutalāy” eṉṟum; oṉṟāy niṉṟu ulakiyaṟ poruḷkaḷi iyakkum tiṟatti, “oṉṟāy, iru, peṇṇāṇāy, kuṇamūṉṟāy, maṟi nāṉkāy, pūtamaviyin tāy, āṟār cuviyāy, ēḻōciyāy, eṭṭut tici tāṉāy” eṉṟum; uyirkaḷōṭu oṉṟākāmalum vēṟā kāmalum, uṭaṉiruntu kaṇṭum, kāṭṭiyum, utaviyaruḷum, mutalvaṉ tiṟatti “uṭaṉāṉāṉ” eṉṟum ittiruppāṭṭil tiruñāṉacampantap perumāṉ aruḷic ceykiṉṟār. ivvāṟu iṟivaṉatu mūvaki niliyiṉiyum tiru varuḷāḷ uṇarntu oruṅkē eṭuttu ōtiya nūl vaṭamoḻiyilō vēṟu moḻikaḷilō itukāṟum kāṇappaṭavilli. ikkaruttuk kaḷi vaṟpuṟuttuvataṟkākattāṉ. āciriyar, pulavar perumakkaḷāl “antam āti” eṉap pukaḻn tōtappeṟṟa mutalvaṉ, “aviyē tāṉēyāy niṟkum” eṉṟu iccūttirattiṉ eṭutta eṭuppilēyē iyampi yaruḷukiṉṟār.
ulakil nilavum uyirkaḷ ovvoṉṟum maṉattāl niṉittalum vāyāṟ collutalum, uṭampāl ceyal ceytalum ceykiṉṟaṉa. iviyāvum viṉiyeṉṟē kūṟappaṭum. ivviṉikaḷ, iṉpam viḷiyumāyiṉ nalviṉiyum, tuṉpam viḷiyumāyiṉ tīviṉiyum eṉa iru vakip peyar peṟum, ivviṉiyum viṉippayaṉum uyirpōla aṟivillātaṉa vātalāl, avi tammic ceyta uyirit tāmē ceṉṟu cērā; viṉiyiyum viṉippayaṉ kaḷiyum kūṭṭuvippatu mutalvaṉ ceyal. viṉikkuriya payaṉiyum, payaṉi uyirkaḷ nukartaṟkuriya uṭal karuvikaḷiyum mutalvaṉ taṉ āṇiyākiya ciṟcattiyāl tōṟṟuvippaṉ. avvāṇi, uyirkaḷiṉ viṉi nukarccik kēṟpa uṭal karuvi mutaliyavaṟṟik  kūṭṭuvatum nīkkuvatum ceyyum. kūṭṭuṅkāl piṟappum, nīkkuṅkāl iṟappu uṇṭākiṉṟaṉa. āṉatu paṟṟiyē, uyirkaḷ āṇiyiṉ iru viṉiyiṉ pōkkuvaravu puriya eṉṟu āciriyar urittaruḷu kiṉṟār. pōkku, iṟappu; varavu, piṟappu.
ivviraṇṭu cūttiraṅkaḷālum, ulakiṟku mutalvaṉ iṟivaṉ eṉṟum, mutaṟkāraṇam māyi yeṉṟum, tuṇik kāraṇam āṇiyākiya aruṭcatti yeṉṟum, ulakap paṭippukkuk kāraṇam uyirkaḷiṭattiluḷḷa malam eṉṟum, ulaka vāḻviṉkaṇ uyirkaḷ viṉi ceykiṉṟaṉa eṉṟum kaṇṭōm. ivvakiyāl, iṟivaṉ, uyir, ulaku eṉṟa mūṉṟum, malam, māyi, viṉi eṉṟa mūṉṟum aṟikiṉṟōm. malam mutaliya mūṉṟiyum pācam eṉṟum, ivaṟṟōṭu toṭakkuṟum uyirip pacu eṉṟum, iṟivaṉip pati eṉṟum kūṟuvatu vaḻakkam. ivaṟṟi uṇarttavē.
“	aviyē tāṉē yāyiru viṉiyiṉ
 	pōkku varavu puriya āṇiyiṉ
 	nīkkam iṉṟi niṟkum aṉṟē”
eṉa aṟivuṟuttukiṉṟār.
3. uyireṉpatu oṉṟu uṇṭeṉpati mūṉṟām cūttirat tāl vaṟpuṟuttukiṉṟār. uyirkku āṉmā eṉṟa peyari vētānta nūlkaḷākiya upaniṭataṅkaḷ kūṟukiṉṟaṉa. nam nāṭṭil camaya neṟiyil niṉṟa aṟiñaruḷ cilar āṉmā eṉa oṉṟu kiṭiyātu eṉṟaṉar; cilar uṭampē āṉmā eṉṟaṉar: cilar intiriyaṅkaḷiyē āṉmā eṉṟaṉar; kaṉaviṉ kaṇ kāṇappaṭum cūkkuma uṭampiyē cilar āṉmā eṉṟaṉar; pirāṇavāyuviyē āṉmā eṉṟaṉar cilar. ivvāṟu cutta piramamē āṉmā eṉṟum, ellāk karuvikaḷum kūṭiya kūṭṭamē āṉmā eṉṟum cilar kūṟi vantaṉar. avarkaḷi muṟiyāka eṭuttu maṟuttu avar kūṟuvaṉavaṟṟiṉ vēṟāka āṉmā eṉṟu oṉṟu uḷatu eṉa āciriyar nilināṭṭu vārāyiṉar.
uṭampi nōkki, uṭal, poṟi, maṉam mutaliya kūṟukaḷākap pakuttu ovvoṉṟiyum uyirallaveṉak kaḻittu oṉṟum āṉmā aṉṟu eṉṟu kaṇṭu āṉmā eṉa oṉṟu illiyeṉpārip pārttu, ivvāṟu kūṟiṭṭu nōkki āṉmā illiyeṉa eṇṇik kūṟuvatuyātō atuvē āṉmā eṉṟu collaluṟṟu. “ilatu eṉṟaliṉ āṉmā uḷatu” eṉṟār. uṭalē āṉmā eṉpāri nōkki, “nīṅkaḷ itu eṉatu uṭal eṉkiṟīrkaḷallavā? eṉatu eṉṟa collukkup poruḷāy uḷḷatu yātu?”  eṉṟu kēṭṭu, uṭaliṉ vēṟāka eṉatu eṉṟum coṟkup poru ḷāy iruppatuvē āṉmā; ataṟku uṭimiyākiya uṭampu āṉmā aṉṟu eṉṟu teruṭṭutaṟku. “eṉatu uṭal eṉṟaliṉ āṉmā uḷatu” eṉṟār. kaṇ mutaliya intiriyaṅkaḷ poruḷkaḷip pārttal mutaliya aṟivuc ceyalic ceyvatāl avvintiriyamē āṉmā eṉpavarkaḷik kaṇṭu, kaṇ mutaliya poṟikaḷ tattamakkuriya oḷi mutaliyavaṟṟik kaṇṭaṟiyumēyaṉṟi, kaṇ ōciyiyum kātu oḷiyiyum ivvāṟē piṟa piṟavaṟṟiyum aṟiyā; iviyē āṉmāvāyiṉ, oṉṟē ellāvaṟṟiyum aṟiya vēṇṭum. avvāṟu aṟiyāmiyāl intiriyam āṉmā ākātu eṉpārāy, “aimpulaṉ aṟitaliṉ āṉmā uḷatu” eṉṟār.
iṉi, cūkkuma vuṭampē āṉmā eṉpāri nōkkik kaṉaviṉkaṇ cūkkuma vuṭampu kāṇum kāṭciyi naṉaviṉ kaṇ tūlavuṭampu kaṇṭatākak kūṟuvatāl, ataṟku vēṟāka oṉṟu niṉṟu kāṇkiṟateṉpatu peṟappaṭum. avvoṉṟē āṉmā eṉa aṟika eṉpārāy, “oṭukkam aṟitaliṉ āṉmā uḷatu” eṉṟār. pirāṇavāyuvē āṉmāvāyiṉ, aḵtu naṉavukkālattil nukarum iṉpat tuṉpaṅkaḷi uṟaṅkuṅ kālattilum pirāṇavāyuviṉ iyakkam iruppatāl nukartal vēṇṭum; avvāṟu nukarcci illāmiyāl pirāṇa vāyu āṉmā aṉṟām. ākavē ataṉiṉ vēṟāka āṉmā uḷatu eṉpār, “kaṇpaṭil uṇṭi viṉi iṉmiyiṉ āṉmā uḷatu” eṉṟār, uṇṭi yeṉṟatu iṉpat tuṉpaṅkaḷi nukarvata.
iṉi, cutta piramamē āṉmā eṉpavarkaḷip pārttu, piramam eṉpatu, ellāvaṟṟiyum oruvar aṟivikka aṟiyāmal oruṅkē oru kuṟiyumillāmal tāṉē aṟiyum citaṉṉiyam eṉṟu collukiṟīrkaḷ. āṉmāvō, tāṉē etiyum aṟiya māṭṭātu, aṟintōr aṟivikka aṟiyum ciṟumiyuṭiyatu; ākavē, piramamē āṉmā eṉpatu poruntā teṉpārāy, “uṇartta uṇartaliṉ āṉmā uḷatu” eṉṟār.
iṉi, poṟi mutaliya ellāvaṟṟiṉ kūṭṭamē āṉmā veṉpārkku, poṟi mutaliya ellām oru pāviyiṭattum kūṭiyuḷḷaṉa; oru piṇattiṉiṭattum uṭampukkuriya kūṟukaḷ ellām kūṭiyirukkiṉṟaṉa. eṉiṉum āṉmāvukkuriya aṟivu viḻivu ceyalkaḷ illi. ākavē akkūṭṭattiṉ vēṟāka āṉmā oṉṟu ivvuṭampiṉuḷ uḷatu eṉṟu kūṟukiṉṟār.poṟi mutaliya karuvikaḷiṉ kūṭṭamākiya uṭampukku mutaṟkāraṇam māyi;ammāyi āṉmā aṉṟu; anta āṉmāvukku inta māyā kāriyamāṉa uṭampu iṭamākum; avvuṭam piṉ ceyal koṇṭu. ataṉuḷ āṉmā iruppatu uṇarappaṭukiṟatu eṉpati viḷakkutaṟku. “māyā iyantira taṉuviṉuḷ āṉmā uḷatu” eṉṟār. māyā iyantira taṉu - māyiyiliruntu tōṟṟuvikkap paṭṭa iyantiram pōṉṟa uṭampu. ikkaruttukkaḷiyē.
“	uḷatuilatu eṉṟaliṉ eṉatuṭal eṉṟaliṉ
 	aimpulaṉ oṭukkam aṟitaliṉ kaṇpaṭil
 	uṇṭiviṉi iṉmiyiṉ uṇartta uṇartaliṉ
 	māyā iyantira taṉuviṉuḷ āṉmā”
eṉṟu kūṟukiṉṟār.
4. mūṉṟām cūttirattil āciriyar, kāṇappaṭāta uyiri uṇṭu eṉpataṟkākak kāṇappaṭum namatu uṭampiyē ārāyntu kāṭṭiṉār. nam uṭampil aṟi karuvi, ceyaṟ karuvi eṉa iruvakik karuvikaḷ uḷḷaṉa. aṟi karuviyi ñāṉēntiriyam eṉṟum, ceyaṟ karuviyik kaṉmēntiriyam eṉṟum vaṭa moḻiyiṟ kūṟuvar. avaṟṟuḷ ñāṉēntiriyam, meyvāy kaṇ mūkku cevi eṉa aintākum. kaṇmēntiriyam, vāy kāl ki pāyu upattam eṉa aintām. mey mutaliya aṟi karuvikaḷāl ulakiyali aṟivatākiya ceyalic ceykiṉṟōm. vāy mutaliya ceyaṟ karuvikaḷāl pēcutal, naṭattal mutaliya ceyalkaḷic ceykiṉṟōm. ik karuvikaṭku vēṟāka, maṉam cittam akaṅkāram puttiyeṉa nāṉku uṭkaruvikaḷ nam uṭampil uṇṭu. maṉam cittam mutaliyavaṟṟi antakkaraṇam eṉa aṟivu nūlkaḷ kūṟum. uṭampu parupporuḷāyk kaṇṇukkut teriyakkūṭiyatu; uyir kaṇṇukkut teriyāta aruvap poruḷ. uṭampiṉ elliyi nīṅkāmal ataṉuḷḷē aṟivāy uṭampiṉ pala vēṟu kūṟukaḷiyum iyakkum iyakkap poruḷāy uyir irukkiṟatu. uṭampiṉ elliyi urumaṇṭalam eṉṟum, aṟivuruvāy nilavum uyirelliyi aṟivu maṇṭalam eṉṟum aṟiñar kūṟuvar. aṟikaruvikaḷum ceyaṟ karuvikaḷum urumaṇṭalattil iruppavi. uṭkaruvikaḷāṉa antak karaṇaṅkaḷ aṟivu maṇṭalattil irukkiṉṟaṉa. uru maṇṭalatti uruvuṭampu eṉṟum. ataṟkuḷ iruppatu antak karaṇaṅ kaḷit taṉṉiṭattē koṇṭa aruvuṭampu eṉṟum, nammuṭiya nūlkaḷ kuṟikkum. āṅkilam jermaṉi mutaliya moḻikaḷip pēcum mēṉāṭṭār lattīṉ moḻiyāl tammuṭiya ārāycciyiṟ kaṇṭa poruḷ kaḷukkup peyariṭṭu vaḻaṅkuvatu pōla nam nāṭṭār vaṭamoḻiyāl ñāṉēntiriyam, kaṉmēntiriyam, antakkaraṇam eṉap peyariṭṭu vaḻaṅkuvar. ākavē, ivi vaṭacollāl kuṟikkappaṭuvatupaṟṟi vaṭanūlārukkut tāṉ inta ñāṉēntiriya mutaliyaṉavuṇṭu; tamiḻarkaṭku illi yeṉṟu kūṟip piṇaṅkuvatu peruntaṉmi yākātu. ivvāṟē innūlil āṅkāṅkuk kāṇappaṭum vaṭacoṟkaḷ āṭci kāraṇamāka mēṟ koṇṭaviyeṉak koḷḷal vēṇṭum.
iṉi, mūṉṟām cūttirattil intiriyaṅkaḷ āṉmā ākā eṉpatu kaṇṭa camayavātikaḷiṟcilar, antakkaraṇamē āṉmā eṉṟu kūṟiṉar. antakkaraṇam āṉmāviṉ ceyalukkuk karuviyākumē tavira āṉmāvākātu eṉpārāy, “antak karaṇam avaṟṟiṉ oṉṟaṉṟu āṉmā  eṉṟu āciriyar nāṉkām cūttirattil kūṟukiṉṟār. kaṇ mutaliya karuvikaḷiṉ vāyilākak kāṇap
paṭuvatip paṟṟuvatu maṉam; ataṉi iṉṉateṉac cintippatu cittam; cintaṉiyiṉkaṇ itutāṉeṉat tuṇivēṉeṉa eḻuvatu akaṅkāram; tuṇintu taṉkaṇ koḷvatu putti. ivaṟṟik karuviyākak koṇṭu āṉmā aṟivup poruḷi aṟintu koḷḷum. ivvakiyil maṉam mutaliya nāṉkum āṉmāvākiya aracaṉukku amiccar pōla niṉṟu aṟivuttuṇi ceykiṉṟaṉa. ikkaruvikaḷ āṉmāvōṭu kūṭi ataṟku amiccut toḻil ceyyum eṉpatu viḷaṅkavē, āciriyar, “avi cantittu niṉṟu amiccaracu ēyppa” eṉṟu kūṟiyaruḷukiṉṟār.
muṉpu kaṇṭa uru maṇṭalamum aṟivu maṇṭalamu mākiya iraṇṭiyum oṉṟāyaṭakki āṉmatattuvam eṉṟu nam camaya nūlkaḷ kūṟukiṉṟaṉa. itaṉiṉ vēṟāka iruvakit tattuvaṅkaḷ uṇṭu; avi, vittiyā tattuvam civa tattuvam eṉappaṭum. vittiyā tattuvam (Spiritual Sphere) nuṇṇuṭampukku uḷḷāy uyiric cūḻntu niṟpatu. civatattuvam uyirkku mēlāyt tiruvaruḷ tōytaṟku vāyilāy (Medium) iruppatu. iṟi vaṉuṭiya aruṭcatti civa tattuvattiṉ vāyilāka āṉmāviṉ aṟivu, viḻivu, ceyal eṉṟa mūvakiyāṉa āṟṟalkaḷiyum kiḷarac ceyyum; civa tattuvattiṉ vāyilāka varum avvaruṭ cattiyē āṉma tattuvattiyum vittiyā tattuvattiyum toḻiṟ paṭuttukiṟatu. vittiyā tattuvattāl uyiriṉuṭiya aṟivu mutaliya āṟṟalkaḷ eḻuntu aṟitaṟkum, viḻitaṟkum, ceytaṟkum muṟpaṭu kiṉṟaṉa. appōtu āṉma tattuvam aṟital mutaliya ceyalkaḷi nikaḻvikkiṉṟatu. āṉmāvākiya uyiric cūḻntu āṉmatattuvamum ataṟkuḷ vittiyā tattuvamum, ataṟkuḷḷāka āṉmāvum irukkiṉṟaṉa. itu paṟṟiyē āṉmatattuvattu intiriyaṅkaḷip puṟakkaraṇam eṉṟum; maṉamutaliyaṉa avaṟṟiṟkuḷḷāka iruppatu paṟṟi akakkaraṇamākiya antakkaraṇam eṉṟum, avaṟṟiṟkum uḷḷēyirukkiṉṟa vittiyā tattuvatti uḷḷantakkaraṇam eṉṟum, avaṟṟiṟkum uḷḷāl irukkiṟa āṉmāvi uḷḷam eṉṟum cāṉṟōr vaḻaṅkukiṉṟaṉar. “ōrttu uḷḷam uḷḷatu uṇariṉ oru taliyāp. pērttuuḷḷa vēṇṭā piṟappu” eṉṟa (357) tirukkuṟaḷil tiruvaḷḷuvar āṉmāvi uḷḷam eṉa vaḻaṅkuvati iṅkē niṉivu kūrtal vēṇṭum.
ivvāṉma vittiyā tattuvaṅkaḷukku uḷḷē irukkum āṉmāvukku aṟivu mutaliya mūvaki āṟṟalum iyalpākavē uṇṭeṉṟālum, mutaṟ cūttirattil kāṭṭappaṭṭa malattāl maṟippuṇṭu kiṭakkiṉṟaṉa. ammalam cempil kaḷimpu pōla iyalpāy āṉmāvil aṉāti toṭarpu koṇṭiruppatāl atu cakacamalam eṉṟu kūṟappaṭum iccakacamalattāl maṟip puṇṭu iruppatu viḷaṅka, āciriyar, “āṉmā cakaca malattu uṇarātu” eṉṟār. cakacam, iyaṟki. malattāl maṟippuṇṭu āṟṟal curuṅkiyirukkum āṉmā, avvāṟṟalic celutti niṉivaṉa niṉintu ceyvaṉa ceytu oḻukutaṟku ittattuvaṅkaḷ karuviyāy iṉṟiyamiyātu vēṇṭappaṭukiṉṟaṉa. ivi ōr aracaṉukku amiccarum piṟa araciyal viṉiyāḷarum pōla āṉmāvukku amintuḷḷaṉa eṉpatu paṟṟiyē “amiccaracu ēyppa” eṉṟār.
iṉi, nammuṭampu, uruvuṭampu (tūlavuṭampu), aruvuṭampu (cūkkuma uṭampu), karuvuṭampu (puriyaṭṭaka vuṭampu), nuṇṇuṭampu (kañcuka vuṭampu), kāraṇa vuṭampu eṉa aintu vakiyāka ittattuvaṅkaḷikkoṇṭu pakuttuk kāṇappaṭukiṟatu. ivi nammuṭampiṉuḷ oṉṟiṟkoṉṟu nuṭpamāy amintuḷḷaṉa. uruvuṭampu toḻiṟpaṭuṅkāl uyir puruva naṭuvil niṟkum eṉṟum, cūkkuma uṭampukkuc celluṅkāl, kaḻuttilum, puriyaṭṭaka vuṭampukkuṭ celluṅkāl itayattilum, kañcuka vuṭampil untiyilum, kāraṇa vuṭampōṭu niṟkiyil untiyiṉkīḻ mūlātārattilum niṟkum; puru naṭu mutaliya iṭaṅkaḷ uyirukku uṇarvelliyākum. puruva naṭuvil niṟkum nili cākkiram (kaṉavu) eṉṟum, kaḻuttil niṟkum nili coppaṉam (kaṉavu) eṉṟum, itayattil niṟkum nili cuḻutti (tuyil) eṉṟum, untiyil niṟkum nili turiyam (uṟakkam) eṉṟum, mūlātārattil niṟkum nili atītam (pēruṟakkam) eṉṟum nūlkaḷiṟ kūṟappaṭukiṉṟaṉa. puruva naṭuviliruntu mūlātāram variyil ittattuvak karuvikaḷi viṭṭup paṭippaṭiyāka nīṅkik kīḻē celluvatum, piṉpu paṭippaṭiyāka mēlēṟip puruva naṭuvukku varuvatum, varumpōtu āṅkāṅku viṭṭa karuvikaḷōṭu kūṭuvatum uyirukku uṭampil uṇṭākum iyakkamākum mēlē kūṟiya aivaki nilikaḷum avatti eṉappaṭum. itupaṟṟiyē “āṉmā añcavattittu” eṉṟu kūṟiyaruḷiṉār. itaṉi nāṉkām cūttiram,
“	antak karaṇam avaṟṟiṉ oṉṟaṉṟu avi
 	cantittatu āṉmāc cakacamalat tuṇarātu
	amiccaracu ēyppa niṉṟu añcavat tittē”
eṉṟu iyampukiṟatu. ivvavattiyil, kīḻ nōkkic cellum avatti (iyakka nili) kīḻālavatti yeṉavum, mūlātārat tiliruntu mēlnōkki varum avatti mēlāla vatti yeṉavum vaḻaṅkum.
iccūttirattāl āṉmāvukku aintavattip paṭuvatu ilakkaṇam eṉpatu peṟappaṭuvatu koṇṭu amintu viṭalākātu antak karaṇaṅkaḷē aṟital eṉṟa toḻilic ceyvatāl avaṟṟiyē uyireṉṟal amiyum eṉpavar evvāṟu maṟukkappaṭukiṉṟārkaḷ eṉpati maṟavātu kaṭip piṭittal vēṇṭum. intak kālattil nilavum mēṉāṭṭu uḷanūlkaḷik kāṇu miṭattu antakkaraṇamē āṉmāvām eṉṟa kūṟṟu valiyuṟuvatupōlat tōṉṟum. uyir avattip paṭuvateṉpatu aṟikaruvikaḷōṭu kūṭuvatum kūṭātu nīṅkuvatumākiya niliyi eytuvateṉṟu kaṇṭōm. uyir antakkaraṇaṅkaḷōṭu kūṭiya vaḻi aṟivu viḷakka muṇṭākiṟatu; kūṭāvaḻi aṟiviṉatu viḷakkam kuṟikiṟatu. antak karaṇaṅkaḷē aṟital eṉṉum ceyali yuṭiyavāyiṉ, āṉmā aṭiyum avatti vēṟu pāṭṭuk kēṟpa aṟivu viḷakkattil mikuvatum kuṟivatum uṇṭātaṟkuk kāraṇamilli. āṉmā kīḻum mēlumāka avatti yeytuṅkāl, niṉṟāṅku niṟkum karuvikaḷ, uyir vantu tōyumpōtu viḷaṅkuvatum nīṅkumpōtu viḷakkam kuṉṟuvatum nikaḻkiṉṟaṉa. itaṉāl aṟivākiya oḷiyum ceyalum āṉmāvukkē uriyavāy iruppatu teḷivākiṟatu. ātalāltāṉ āṉmā antak karaṇaṅkaḷākiya avaṟṟiṉ oṉṟaṉṟu eṉpatu vaṟpuṟuttap paṭukiṟatu.
5. antakkaraṇaṅkaḷiṉ vēṟāyc cakacamalattāl uṇarvu maṟikkappaṭṭu uṭal karuvikaḷōṭu kūṭi niṟkum eṉappaṭṭa āṉmā kīḻum mēlumāka aivaki yavatti eytuvāṉēṉ? avatti eytuṅkāl, karuvikaḷōṭu kūṭi aṟivu viḷakkam peṟuvatum avaṟṟōṭu kūṭāta vaḻi aṟivu oṭuṅkutalum eṟṟukku? eṉṟu cila aiyaṅkaḷ nam niṉivil eḻukiṉṟaṉa. inta aiyaṅkaḷi eṇṇi ārāyntōr, ōr uṇmiyik kaṇṭaṉar; ulakiṭit tōṉṟum poruḷkaḷ aṉittum iyaṅkiya vaṇṇam irukkiṉṟaṉa. parup poruḷiṉ iyakkam namakkut terikiṟatu; nuṇporuḷiṉ iyakkam pulaṉāvatilli. pulaṉākāmi koṇṭu avaṟṟiṉ kaṇ iyakkam illi yeṉṟu kūṟamuṭiyātu; ataṉic cila viññāṉikaḷ nuṇṇiya karuvikaḷāl kāṭṭiyumirukkiṉṟaṉar. itaṉāl ellāp poruḷiṭattum iyakkam uṇṭu eṉpatu teḷivākiyirukkiṟatu. uyirp poruḷilum ivviyakkam kāṇappaṭukiṟatu. enta iyakkamum tāṉē uṇṭākātu eṉpatu potu uṇmi. iyakkamellām kāriyamātalāl, ataṟkuk kāraṇamāy iyakkuvatu oṉṟu uṇṭu. avvāṟē uyiri iyakkuvatum oṉṟu uṇṭu. aḵtu iṟivaṉatu tiruvaruḷ eṉṟu aṟiñar kaṇṭuḷḷaṉar. ittiruvaruḷ, karuvikaḷōṭu kūṭi avivēṟu tāṉvēṟu eṉa niṉiyātu aviyē tāṉ eṉa mayakkum āṉmāvi, mēlum kīḻum iyakkiyum, karuvikaḷiṟ pirittum kūṭṭiyum uṇmiyaṟivu peṟa utavukiṟatu.
ivvāṟu taṉakku tōṉṟāt tuṇiyāy uṭaṉiruntu utavipuriyum mutalvaṉatu aruḷi āṉmā uṇarvatilli; avaṉatu aruḷiyiṉṟit tāṉ oṉṟum ceytu koḷḷa muṭiyāta ēḻi eṉpatiyum aḵtu eṇṇuvatilli. curuṅkac collu vōmāyiṉ, uyir taṉṉiyum aṟivatilli; taṉakkut tōṉṟāt tuṇiyāy uṭaṉiṉṟutavum tiruvaruḷ mutalvaṉiyum aṟiva tilli. āṉmāvukku mey vāy mutaliya aṟikaruvikaḷ uḷḷaṉa. avi tattamakku uriya poruḷkaḷik kaṇṭu aṟutiyiṭṭu uyirkku uṇarttukiṉṟaṉa; āṉāl avi tammiyē aṟivatilli; poruḷik kāṇumāṟum ōciyik kēṭkumāṟum celuttum uyiriyēṉum akkaṇ mutaliya karuvikaḷ aṟikiṉṟaṉavō eṉiṉ atuvum kiṭiyātu. atupōlavē uyirkaḷ tammiyō tamakkut tōṉṟāt tuṇiyāy niṉṟu utavum tiru varuḷiyō aṟiyāvā kiṉṟaṉa. itaṉi āciriyar, “viḷampiya vuḷḷattu mey vāy kaṇ mūkku aḷan taṟintu aṟiyā; āṅku avi pōlat tām tam uṇarviṉ tami aruḷ” eṉṟu urikkiṉṟār. uḷḷam eṉṟatu, āṉma vittiyā tattuvaṅkaṭku uḷḷē aruvāy āṟṟal vaṭivāy irukkum āṉmāvi eṉa aṟika.
tammāl aṟiyappaṭāta mutalvaṉatu tiruvaruḷāl uyirkaḷ aṟivu viḻivu ceyalvakikaḷil evvāṟu toḻiṟpaṭukiṉṟaṉa eṉiṉ, kāntattiṉ muṉṉē niṟkum irumpu eṅṅaṉam acikiṟatō aṅṅaṉamē mutalvaṉ tiruvaruḷiṉmuṉ uyirkaḷ aṟivatu viḻivatu mutaliya ceyalkaḷic ceykiṉṟaṉa. irumpu acivatum kāntam aciyātu iruppatum pōla, tiruvaruḷiṉmuṉ uyirkaḷ aṟivatu mutaliyaṉa ceyya mutalvaṉ oru vikāramum illāmal iruppāṉ. ikkaruttu viḷaṅka āciriyar, “kāntam kaṇṭa pacācat taviyē” eṉṟār. pacācam - irumpu. itaṉi,
“	viḷampiya uḷḷattu meyvāy kaṇmūkku
 	aḷantaṟintu aṟiyā āṅkavi pōlat
 	tāntam uṇarviṉ tamiyaruḷ,
 	kāntam kaṇṭa pacācat taviyē.”
eṉṟu aintām cūttiram kūṟukiṟatu.
mey vāy kaṇ mūkku cevi eṉṟa karuvikaḷ āṉmāviṉ tuṇiyāl poruḷkaḷi aṟikiṉṟaṉa; avvāṟu aṟintum, aṟikiṉṟa tammiyum tammi aṟiyac ceyyum āṉmāviyum aṟivatilli; avaṟṟip pōlavē āṉmākkaḷum tiruvaruḷ tuṇiyāl ceyvaṉa ceytu, ceyviṉiyiṉ payaṉi nukar kiṉṟaṉa. avvāṟu nukarntum, nukarkiṉṟa tammiyum, tamakkut tōṉṟāt tuṇiyāy uṭaṉiṉṟu iyakkum mutalvaṉ tiruvaruḷiyum aṟivatilli! tiruvaruḷ iyakka iyaṅkum āṉmāviṉ iyakkam kāntattiṉ muṉpu niṉṟa irumpu iyaṅkuvatu pōlum iyakkamām. appaṭi iyaṅkiṉum caliyāta kāntam pōla, mutalvaṉ calippiṉṟi niruvikāriyāy iruppaṉ eṉpatu itaṉāl uṇarttappaṭum poruḷ.
6. kaṇ kātu mutaliyavaṟṟāl kāṇakkūṭiya parup poruḷ kaḷiyum ōci mutaliya nuṇ poruḷ kaḷiyum kāṇkiṉṟōm. ikkaruvikaḷāl aṟiyap paṭuvaṉa aṉit tum ulakiyaṟ poruḷkaḷē yākum. ivaṟṟi maṉattiṉkaṇ vittuc cintippatāl peṟappaṭum uṇarvup poruḷum ulakiyaṟ poruḷkaḷi aṭippaṭiyākak koṇṭaṉa vātalāl ulakiyal uṇarvupporuḷkaḷē.
mutaṟ cūttirattiṟ kaṇṭavāṟu ivvulakamē nili pēṟillātatākum. niliyillāta poruḷi acattu eṉṟum, niliyuṭiya poruḷic cattu eṉṟum kūṟuvar. meyyic catteṉṟum, poyyi acatteṉṟum kūṟuvatuṇṭu. itu paṟṟiyē “kāṇpa tellām poy” eṉṟa paḻamoḻiyum makkaḷiṭiyē nilavukiṟatu. kāṇpateṉpatu, kaṇṇāṟ kāṇpatu, kātāṟ kēṭpatu vāyāṟ cuvikkappaṭuvatu mutaliyavaṟṟip potuvākak kuṟikkum. ivvāṟu kaṇ mutaliya aṟikaruvikaḷāl aṟiyappaṭuvaṉa vellām uruvum aruvumākiya ulakiyaṟ poruḷkaḷēyāy acattāy irukkiṉṟaṉa. evvakiyālum aṟiyappaṭāta poruḷē kiṭiyātu. oru poruḷ evvāṟṟālum aṟiyappaṭā teṉṟāl, appaṭi oru poruḷē illi yeṉpatē karuttu. avaṉ, avaḷ, atu eṉap pakuttuk kāṇappaṭum ulakiyaṟ poruḷkaḷ uruvuṭiyaṉa vāyiṉum uruvillaṉa vāyiṉum eppaṭiyum avi acattākavē uḷḷaṉa. mutalvaṉākiya paramporuḷ nammuṭiya aṟivāl aṟiyappaṭuvāṉāyiṉ, aṟiyappaṭum ēṉi ulakiyaṟ poruḷ pōla acattāvāṉ. evvāṟṟālum aṟiyappaṭā toḻiyumāyiṉ, param poruḷ oṉṟu illi yeṉṟē muṭiyum. āciriyar ik karuttiyē mutalvaṉākiya paramporuḷ, “uṇar uru eṉiṉ acattu; uṇarātu eṉiṉ iṉmi” eṉṟu aruḷiṉār aḵtāvatu iṟivaṉ nam karuvikaḷāl uṇarappaṭum poruḷ eṉiṉ, acattāvāṉ; uṇarappaṭāṉ eṉiṉ avaṉ eṉṟum evviṭattum illāta poruḷām; aḵtāvatu cūṉiyam eṉpatām.
paramporuḷ oṉṟu uṇṭeṉpatu mutaṟ cūttirattāl tuṇiyappaṭṭatu. ākavē, aḵtu uḷporuḷākiya cattu eṉṟu kūṟappaṭum. kāṇappaṭum nam uṭampikkoṇṭu kāṇappaṭāta uyirākiya nām uṇṭeṉpati mūṉṟām cūttirattāl tuṇin tuḷḷōm. uyirum, paramporuḷ pōla aṉātiyē niṉṟu nilavuvatu; aṟivu viḻivu ceyal eṉṟa āṟṟalkaḷiyuṭiyatu; ataṉāl uyirum cattu eṉṟu kūṟappaṭum. paramporuḷākiya cattu, uyirākiya cattup poruḷāl oruvakiyil uṇarappaṭuvatum oruvakiyil uṇarap paṭāmiyum uḷḷa cattākiṟatu; oruvāṟṟāl uṇarap paṭuvatāl acattāvatum, uṇarappaṭāmiyāl cūṉiya māvatum illi. itaṉi āciriyar, “irutiṟa ṉallatu civacattām eṉa iraṇṭu vakiyiṉ ulaku icikkum” eṉṟu aruḷukiṉṟār. irutiṟaṉ, acattāvatum cūṉiya māvatumākiya iraṇṭu tiṟaṉ; iraṇṭu vaki, oruvāṟṟāl uṇarap paṭuvatum oruvāṟṟāl uṇarap paṭāmiyumākiya iruvaki, ulakiyalaṟivum uyiraṟivum koṇṭu uṇarumiṭattu mutalvaṉākiya paramporuḷ uṇarap paṭuvatilli yeṉṟum, apparamporuḷiṉ tiruvaruḷ aṟivu koṇṭu nōkkiṉ apparamporuḷ uṇarappaṭum eṉṟum meyyuṇarvāl paramporuḷik kaṇṭu iṉpuṟṟa cāṉṟōr kūṟukiṉṟaṉar. “col teriyāp poruḷ cōtikkappāl niṉṟa cōtiyāṉ” eṉṟu tiruñāṉacampantarum, “kāṇpārār kaṇṇutalāyk kāṭṭākkālē” eṉṟu tirunāvukkaracarum kūṟiyaruḷuvatāl mutalvaṉākiya civaparamporuḷ oruvāṟṟāl uṇarappaṭuvatum uṇarappaṭāmiyum, “aruḷālē meyp poruḷik kāṇum vitiyuṭiyēṉ” eṉṟu kārik kālammiyārum. “avaṉaruḷē kaṇṇakak kāṇiṉallāl” eṉṟu tirunāvukkaracarum kūṟiyaruḷuvatāl, tiruvaruḷ aṟivu oṉṟē civaparam poruḷik kāṇṭaṟkut tuṇiyām eṉpatum civat tirumuṟikaḷil vaṟpuṟuttappaṭukiṉṟaṉa. itupaṟṟiyē, “iraṇṭu vakiyiṉ icikkumaṉ ulaku” eṉṟār. ulaku eṉpatu tiruvaruḷē kaṇṇākak koṇṭu iṟivaṉik kaṇṭu iṉpuṟṟa uyarntōrik kuṟikkum. mēlē kūṟiya cāṉṟōrkaḷi āciriyar, ulaku eṉa uyarttik kūṟiyuḷḷār. 
“	uṇaruru acattu eṉiṉ uṇarātu iṉmiyiṉ
	irutiṟaṉ allatu civacat tāmeṉa
 	iraṇṭu vakiyiṉ icikkumaṉ ulakē.” 
eṉpatu ikkarutti yuṇarttum cūttiram.
iṅkē kūṟiya ulakiyalaṟivu pācañāṉam eṉṟum, uyiraṟivu pacuñāṉam eṉṟum, tiruvaruḷ aṟivu civañāṉam (patiñāṉam) eṉṟum vaḻaṅkum. ulakiyalaṟivu kalvi kēḷvikaḷāl uṇṭākum ceyaṟkiyaṟivu; itu pācañāṉam, uyiraṟivu uṇmi yaṟiveṉap paṭum iyaṟkiyaṟivu; itu pacuñāṉam, paramporuḷ pacupāca ñāṉaṅkaḷāl aṟiyap paṭātu, civa ñāṉattāl aṟiyappaṭuvatu paṟṟic civacattu eṉṟu āciriyarāl kuṟikkappaṭukiṟatu.
iccūttirattāl, civa paramporuḷ iyaṟkiyum ceyaṟkiyu mākiya nam aṟivelliyik kaṭantu niṟpiṉum, ataṉāl aruḷappaṭum civañāṉattāl āṉmākkaḷākiya nām ataṉi aṟital kūṭum eṉpatu vaṟpuṟuttap peṟuvatu kāṇka.
7. iṟivaṉ uyir ulaku eṉṟa mūṉṟaṉuḷ, iṟivaṉic civacattu eṉṟum, ulaki acatteṉṟum muṉṉic cūttirattāl aṟintōm. cattākiya civam acatti nōkkumāyiṉ, atu niliyiṉṟip pōkum marattāl ceyyappaṭṭa yāṉi yoṉṟi atu ceyyavalla oru marattaccaṉ kāṇpāṉāyiṉ, avaṉukkuc cattākiya maram pulaṉākumē aṉṟi accatākiya yāṉi vaṭivam pulappaṭātu. atu pōlavē, cattākiya civattiṉ muṉ acattākiya ulakam nillātu maṟintoḻiyum, acattākiya ulakam taṉṉi liyil aṟiyum aṟivu illātatu; ataṉāl atu cattākiya civatti aṟiyātu. itaṉiyē āciriyar, “cattiṉ etir yāviyum cūṉiyam; ākaliṉ, cattu aṟiyātu; acattu ilatu ākaliṉ aṟiyātu” eṉṟār. yāviyum eṉṟatu acattākiya ulakiyaṟ poruḷkaḷiyākum. avi tōṟṟu vikkattōṉṟi niṉṟu aḻivaṉa vātalāl acattākum. cattākiya civattiṉ muṉ kāriyamākiya avi nillātu pōtu koṇṭu cūṉiyam eṉṟu āciriyar kūṟiyaruḷukiṉṟār. acattu ilatu eṉṟatu acattākiya ulakiyaṟ poruḷ uyirillātatu; ataṉāl aṟivillātatu; ākavē, ataṟku etaṉiyum aṟiyum tiṟam illi eṉpatām.
ivvakiyāl, iṟivaṉ ulaku eṉṟa iraṇṭum cattum acattumāy oṉṟaṉi yoṉṟu aṟiyā eṉpatu kāṇum namakku. ivviraṇṭiṉ vēṟākiya uyir, cattākiya civattiyum acattākiya ulakattiyum aṟiyum pōlum eṉṟa aiyamuṇṭākiṟatu. anta aiyatti nīkkavē āciriyar, iraṇṭiṉiyum aṟiyavallatu āṉmā eṉpārāy, “iru tiṟaṉ aṟivuḷatu iraṇṭalā āṉmā” eṉṟu ēḻām cūttirattāl kūṟukiṉṟār. irutiṟaṉ aṟivuḷatu eṉpatu, cattum acattumākiya iraṇṭiṉiyum aṟiyum aṟivu uḷḷatu eṉṟum, iraṇṭiṉālum aṟiyum aṟivuḷḷatu eṉṟum, iraṇṭiṉiṭattum niṉṟu aṟiyum aṟivuḷḷatu eṉṟum poruḷ koḷḷappaṭum. cattākiya civattāl aruḷappaṭum civañāṉattāl uyir accivatti aṟiyum aṟivu peṟukiṟatu; acattākiya karuvi karaṇaṅkaḷik koṇṭē ulakiyalaṟivi aṟiyum aṟivuṭiyatākiṉṟatu. ataṉāl irutiṟaṉāl aṟiyum aṟivuḷatu eṉṟu poruḷ koḷḷappaṭṭatu. civattiṉkaṇ tōyntu civamāy niṉṟu civapōkatti aṟintu nukarvatum, acattākiya ulakiyalil tōyntu ulakapporuḷāy iyintu ulakiyal iṉpatti aṟintu nukarvatum ceyvatāl, uyirākiya āṉmā iraṇṭiṉpālum aṟiyum aṟivu uḷḷatu eṉṟu poruḷ koḷḷappaṭṭatu. ikkaruttellām viḷaṅkavē.
“	yāviyum cūṉiyam cattetir, ākaliṉ
 	cattē aṟiyātu acattu ilatu aṟiyātu
	irutiṟaṉ aṟivuḷatu iraṇṭalā āṉmā.”
eṉṟu āciriyar aṟivuṟuttukiṉṟār.
itaṉkaṇ cattākiya civattiyum acattākiya ulakat tiyum vēṟupaṭavittum, tuṇiyākak koṇṭum, iṭamāka niṉṟum aṟiyum uyirākiya āṉmā, cattākiya civamum aṉṟu; acattākiya ulakiyaṟ poruḷum aṉṟu eṉa aṟikiṉṟōm. itapaṟṟiyē āciriyar, āṉmāvi iraṇṭalā āṉmā eṉṟu kūṟiyaruḷiṉār. cattaṉṟāyiṉum cattōṭiyintu cattākiya civattiyum, acattōṭu kūṭi acattāy niṉṟu acattiyum aṟiyum taṉmiyuṭiyatāka iruppatāl inta āṉmāvic catacattu eṉṟu cāṉṟōr kūṟuvārāyiṉar. “cattu acattu aṟivatu āṉmā, tāṉ cattum acattum aṉṟu. nittamāyc catacattāki niṉṟiṭum” eṉṟu aruṇanti civaṉārum, “catacattām uyir” eṉṟu umāpati civaṉārum viḷakkiṉār.
8. catacatteṉappaṭum āṉmākkaḷākiya nām acattākiya uṭampōṭu kūṭi acattākiya ulakiyalil aḻunti ulakiyal vāḻkkiyi naṭattukiṉṟōm. ivvāḻkkiyil kaṇ,kātu mutaliya karuvikaḷāl aṟivaṉa aṟivatum, ki, kāl, vāy mutaliya karuvikaḷāl  ceyvaṉa ceyvatum nammuṭiya ceyalkaḷākiṉṟaṉa. icceyalkaḷil īṭupaṭuṅ kāl namakku aṟivu viḷakka muṇṭākiṟatu. appōtu iṉpamum uṇṭākiṟatu. āṉmā anta iṉpattukku aṭimiyāki, anta acat tākiya karuvikaḷ kāṭṭum kāṭciyiyē virumpi avi iyaṅkum neṟiyilēyē cellukiṟatu. ulakiyal vāḻkkiyōṭu toṭarpuṇṭāṉatāl viḷiyum payaṉkiṭṭātu pōkiṟatu. āṉmā, taṉakkum acattākiya ulakukkum uḷḷa vēṟupāṭṭiyalpi yeṇṇi, ulaku uṭal karuvikaḷik karuviyākak koṇṭu peṟutaṟkuriya payaṉi eytavēṇṭum. appayaṉ uṇmi aṟivāl uṇarappaṭum. anta aṟiviyum mutalvaṉ uṭaṉāy niṉṟu uṇartti varukiṉṟāṉ. ivvakiyāl, uṇmi yaṟiviṉ viḷakkattāl, uṭal karuvi ulaka vāḻvukaḷip payaṉ koṇṭu varum āṉmāvukku, malattāl taṭiyuṇṭirukkum ciṟumi keṭat toṭaṅkukiṟatu anta niliyil uṇṇiṉṟu tōṉṟāt tuṇiyāy uṇmi aṟivik kāṭṭiyaruḷum mutalvaṉ, ñāṉāciriyaṉ uruvil āṉmākkaḷiṉ etiril vantu uṇmiyaṟivākiya civañāṉatti nalkuvaṉ. māṇikkavācakarukkut tirupperuntuṟiyil kurunta marattiṉkīḻ eḻuntaruḷiyatum, meykaṇṭa tēvarukkut tiruveṇṇeynallūril parañcōti muṉivar eḻuntaruḷiyatum pōla eḻuntaruḷi vantu civañāṉatti utavuvāṉ.
“vēntaṉ oruvaṉuṭi makaṉ vēṭar kūṭṭattiṟ paṭṭu avar taruvaṉavaṟṟip peṟṟu vaḷarntuvantāṉ. vaḷarntavaṉ, taṉṉi ōr aracaṉ makaṉ eṉpati maṟantu, vēṭar vayamē niṉṟu taṉṉi avvēṭariṉ vēṟallēṉ eṉṟē niṉikkaluṟṟāṉ. avaṉ vaḷarcciyip pārttuk koṇṭē vanta vēntaṉ, avaṉukkuc coṉṉāl kēṭṭut teḷintu accol vaḻi niṟkak kūṭiya cevvi varuvatu kaṇṭāṉ. uṭaṉē tāṉum oru vēṭaṉ uruvil avaṉetiril vantu avaṉōṭu collāṭi, “nī vēṭaṉ alli; vēṭarkaḷiṭiyē vaḷarntu uṉṉi iṉṉāṉeṉa aṟiyātu maṟantu viṭṭāy; nī oru vēntaṉuṭiya makaṉ” eṉṟu collit teruṭṭiṉāṉ. uṭaṉē avaṉ, vēntaṉ iyalpukaḷik kēṭṭaṟintu vēntaṉuṭiya paṇpukaḷi yeṇṇi vēntaṉāy viḷakkamuṟṟāṉ” eṉpatu pōlum varalāṟṟic collic civañāṉam uṇarttavarum ñāṉāciriyaṉ, āṉmākkaḷākiya nammit teruṭṭat toṭaṅkuvaṉ. “nāṉ mēlē coṉṉa aracaṉ makaṉ āṉmāvākiya nī; vēṭar kūṭṭam eṉṟatu, uṉakkuk karuvikaḷāka aminta aimpulaṉkaḷākum; avvaracaṉ makaṉ pōlavē āṉmāvākiya nī uṉṉi maṟantu akkaruvikaḷiṉ vayamē niṉṟu oḻukukiṉṟāy; nī ivaṟṟiṉ vēṟu; aṟivē uṉakku uru; uṇmiyaṟi viṉpamākiya civapōkatti nukartaṟkē  nī uriyi; civāṉanta ñāṉa vaṭivu koṇṭu civa param poruḷiṉ tiruvaṭi nīḻalil eṉṟum poṉṟāta iṉpam peṟutal vēṇṭum” eṉṟu aṟivuṟuttuvaṉ. itaṉi āciriyar, “aimpula vēṭariṉ vaḷarntu ayarn taṉi eṉat tam mutal kuruvumāy uṇartta” eṉṟu aruḷukiṉṟār. tam mutal eṉṟatu āṉmākkaḷukku mutal vaṉākiya iṟivaṉik kuṟikkum. kuruvumāy eṉṟatu, itaṟku muṉ ṉellām uyirkkuyirāy uṭaṉiruntu uḷḷē aṟivaruḷi varuvatēyaṉṟi kuruvākavum varukiṉṟāṉ eṉpatik kuṟikkiṟatu. coṉṉa collik kēṭṭu accolvaḻi niṟkakkūṭiya cevvi varak kaṇṭu vēntaṉ aṟivuṟuttiyatu pōla, āṉ mākkaḷākiya namakku ñāṉavuri kēṭṭu ataṉ vaḻiyē niṟkakkūṭiya takuti varumpōtutāṉ ñāṉāciriyaṉ varuvaṉ. ceyyappaṭum tavattāl tāṉ āṉmākkaḷukku attakutiyum cevviyum uṇṭākum. ātalāl, “tavattiṉil uṇartta” eṉa āciriyar aṟivuṟut taruḷukiṉṟār.
araca kumaraṉukku vēṭar kūṭṭattiṉ toṭarpu, vēntaṉ col kēṭṭu vēntākum paṇpu peṟutaṟkuk karuviyāyiruntu, ataṉip peṟat talippaṭṭatum viṭupaṭṭatu pōla, āṉmā ñāṉāciriyaṉatu ñāṉavuri kēṭṭu ñāṉaneṟi yoḻukum cevvi peṟutaṟku uṭal karuvi mutaliya acattiṉ toṭarpu karuviyākiṟatu. ñāṉam peṟṟu ñāṉaneṟi niṟkat talippaṭuṅkāl āṉmā acattiṉ toṭarpi viṭṭuviṭukiṟatu. “niliyāta ulakiyalpu kaṇṭu niliyā vāḻkki yallēṉ”  eṉṟu aṟintu tirunāvukkaracar aṟat tuṇintatu pōla, āṉmā, uṭal, karuvi ulaku mutaliya acattiṉ vēṟātalāl ataṉōṭu uḷatākiya toṭarpu aṟṟoḻukuvatē atu ceyyattakkatu. “oṉṟalāvuyir vāḻkkiyi niṉintiṭṭu uṭal taḷarntu arumā niti iyaṟṟi, eṉṟum vāḻalām emakkeṉap pēcam ituvum poy” eṉṟu nampiyārūrar niṉintu acattiṉ toṭarpi viṭṭatu pōlavum, “eṇṇa mō uṭal, vāy, mūk koṭu, cevi, kaṇ eṉṟivi niṉkaṇē vittu maṇṇiṉ mēl aṭiyēṉ vāḻkilēṉ” eṉṟu maṇivācakar karuti yoḻukiyatu pōlavum oḻukuvataṉāl acattiṉ toṭarpu viṭum.
āṉmāvukkuc catacattu eṉṟa peyaruṇṭāṉatu, cat tōṭum acattōṭum toṭarpuṟṟu niṟkum iyalpiṉāl tāṉ, cattu acattu eṉṟa iraṇṭiloṉṟic cārntu niṟpatu uyirkku iyalpēyaṉṟi vēṟē taṉittu niṟkum iyalpu ataṟkuk kiṭiyātu. catacattākiya āṉmā acattiṉ toṭarpu viṭṭavaḻi, cattākiya civattiyē ceṉṟu cārum. ūcalāṭum oruvaṉukku ataṉ kayiṟu aṟumāyiṉ avaṉ tariyic cārvatu pōla, āṉmā acattiṉ toṭarpuviṭṭāl cattākiya cittiyē cārum. cattāntaṉ miyālum cittāntaṉmi (aṟivuṭiyatu eṉṉum taṉmi) yālum āṉmāvukkuc cattākiya civam anniyamaṉṟu; ataṉāl āṉmā civattiyē ceṉṟu cārum eṉpatu teḷivām. āṉmāvukku ēṉi uṭal karuvi mutaliya acattu anniyam. acattic cārntu atuvatu vāy iyintirunta pōtum, āṉmā acattiṉ vēṟāka iraṇṭu paṭṭu niṉṟatupōliṉṟi cattākiya civattic cārntu oṉṟukiṟapōtu ataṉkaṇ oṉṟi uṭaṉāy niṟkum. itaṉi uṇarttuvataṟku āciriyar, “viṭṭu aṉṉiyam iṉmiyiṉ araṉkaḻalcelumē” eṉṟār itaṉi,
“	aimpula vēṭariṉ ayarntaṉi vaḷarnteṉat
 	tammutal kuruvumāyt tavattiṉil uṇarttaviṭṭu
	aṉṉiyam iṉmiyiṉ araṉkaḻal celumē.”
eṉa varuvatu kāṇka. araṉkaḻal ceṉṟu cērnta tiruñāṉa campantaric cēkkiḻār perumāṉ, “pōtanili muṭinta vaḻippukku oṉṟi yuṭaṉāṉār” eṉṟum, tirunāvukkaracari. “naṇṇariya civāṉanta ñāṉa vaṭivēyāki aṇṇalār cēvaṭik kīḻ āṇṭa aracu amarntiruntār” eṉṟum kūṟuvataṉāl itu naṉku teḷiyappaṭum. “civāṉanta ñāṉa vaṭivēyāki ” eṉṟatu oṉṟutal; “amarntiruntār” eṉṟatu uṭaṉātal. ivvāṟu oṉṟiyuṭaṉātal eṉpatiyē vaṭacollaṟiñar, ananniya mātal eṉṟum attuvitamāyk kalattal eṉṟum kūṟukiṉṟaṉar.
9. āṉmāvākiya uyirukku iyaṟkiyum ceyaṟkiyumāka iruvakiyaṟivu uṇṭu. iyaṟkiyaṟivu eṉpatu, uṭal karuviyāka uyiriṭattuṇṭākum aṉupava aṟivu. ceyaṟki yaṟiveṉpatu kalvi kēḷvikaḷiṉāl uṇṭākum aṟivu. iyaṟki yaṟivillāta viṭattuc ceyaṟkiyaṟivu ettuṇivariṉum payaṉpaṭātu. “vāyttut taṉakku iyalpāy vanta aṟivu illārkkuc cāttiraṅkaḷ eṉṉa ceyyum” eṉṟu cāṉṟōrkaḷ kūṟuvatu kāṇalām. iyaṟkiyaṟivu uṭaṟ kūṟṟālum, cūḻniliyālum, kuṭivaravālum uḷatāvatu; ceyaṟkiyaṟivu kaṟkappaṭum kalvi vakiyālum, kēḷvi vakiyālum uḷatāvatu. iyaṟkiyaṉupava vaṟivip pacuñāṉa meṉṟum, ceyaṟki yaṟivip pācañāṉa meṉṟum civa nūlkaḷ kūṟum. oruvaṉ pacuñāṉattāṟ kaṇṭa kāṭcikaḷiyē nūl vaṭivil vittu viṭiṉ, atu taṉṉip payilum piṟarkkup pācañāṉamāki viṭum. pāca ñāṉattāl peṟuvatu pala aṟiñarkaḷ kaṇṭuritta pacu ñāṉat tokutiyē. vētam ākamam mutaliya ellā vaṟṟālum uṇṭākum aṟivu pācañāṉamē yākum. uṭaliḷḷa pala karuvikaḷiṉ vāyilākat tokuvatu pacu ñāṉam; palaruṭiya pacuñāṉak kāṭcikaḷi oruṅkē tokuttu nalkuvatu pācañāṉam. ākavē, pacupācañāṉaṅkaḷ eṉpavi āṉmākkaḷ tammuṭiya uṭal karuvi ulakam mutaliyavaṟṟāl peṟum aṟivu eṉpatu tuṇiyappaṭum. ivvuṭal mutaliya karuvikaḷ acattu; acattu eṉavē avaṟṟiṟ keṉat taṉiyē aṟivilli yeṉpatu viḷaṅkum. acattu aṟivila tākaliṉ, cattākiya civatti aṟiyātu eṉṟu āciriyar muṉpē aruḷicceytuḷḷār. ataṉāl, anta acattut tuṇi ceyya vanta pacupācañāṉaṅkaḷ civatti aṟitaṟkut tuṇiyākā eṉpatu teḷiya viḷaṅku kiṟatu. itu paṟṟiyē āciriyar “ūṉakkaṇ pācam uṇa rāp patiyi” eṉṟār. pācam eṉṟatu pācañāṉam ūṉakkaṇ eṉṟatu. acattākiya kaṇ mutaliya karuvi koṇṭu aṟiya varum pacuñāṉam. kaṇ mutaliya uṭaṟ karuvikaḷ acattākiya ūṉam uṭiya vātalāl, avaṟṟāl peṟappaṭum ñāṉam. ūṉakkaṇ eṉappaṭṭatu. āṉatu paṟṟiyē, acattākiya uṭampu eṉṟaṟkut tiruñāṉa cam pantar. “ūṉamām uṭampu” eṉṟum, “ūṉattiruḷ” eṉṟum aṟivuṟuttiyuḷḷār. kaṇṇeṉṟatu aṟivu. pacupāca ñāṉaṅkaḷāl civaparamporuḷip pārkka muṭiyātu. pati eṉṟatu cattākiya civaparam poruḷi eṉa aṟital vēṇṭum.
pacupāca ñāṉaṅkaḷāl kāṇappaṭāta mutalvaṉ avaṉatu tiruvaruḷē kaṇṇākak kāṇpārkkuk kāṭciyaḷippaṉ. ataṉā laṉṟō mutalvaṉukkuc civacattu eṉpatum oru peyarāyiṟṟu. cattātalāl uṇarappaṭāteṉavum, civacattātalāl civañāṉam oṉṟiṉālē uṇarappaṭu meṉavum muṉṉē kūṟiyirukkiṉṟār. civañāṉam ñāṉāciriyarāl aruḷappaṭukiṟatu. civañāṉamē kaṇṇākak koṇṭu puṟakkaruvikaḷik koḷḷāmal akakkaruvi yākiya cintiyil viḷaṅkum aṟiviṉ kaṇṇē ārāytalē vēṇṭuvatu eṉpati nām teḷikiṉṟōm. itu teḷiyavē. āciriyar “ūṉakkaṇ pācam uṇarāp patiyi, ñāṉak kaṇṇiṉil cinti nāṭi” eṉṟu aruḷic ceytuḷḷār. ūṉak kaṇṇum pācamum uṇaramuṭiyāta patiyi ñāṉak kaṇ koṇṭu cintiyiṉkaṇ nāṭuka eṉpatu itaṉ poruḷ; aḵtāvatu pacuñāṉattālum, pācañāṉattālum uṇara muṭiyāta iṟivaṉi avaṉatu tiruvaḷākiya civañāṉattāl cintiyiṉkaṇ ārāyntu uṇara vēṇṭum eṉpatu ‘kūṟuka’ eṉpatik ‘kūṟi’ eṉṟu cāṉṟōr vaḻaṅkuvatu pōla, nāṭuka eṉpati nāṭi eṉṟu vaḻaṅkukiṉṟār. ikkaruttiyē aruṇanti civaṉār. “pācañāṉat tālum pacu ñāṉattālum pārppariya paramparaṉip patiñāṉattālē nēcamoṭum uḷḷattē nāṭi” eṉṟu kūṟukiṉṟār. iṅkē avar  nāṭi eṉpataṟku ārāyka eṉṟu poruḷ koḷḷāmal ārāyntu eṉṟē koḷḷukiṉṟār. maṟṟu civañāṉa muṉivarō, ‘nāṭuka’ eṉṟē poruḷ collukiṉṟār. muṉivar uriyē aṟiñar aṉivarālum īṇṭuk koḷḷappaṭukiṟatu.”
ñāṉāciriyaṉ tiruvaruḷ ñāṉatti nalkap peṟṟāl, āṉmāvākiya nam aṟivukku uṭal karuvi ulakukaḷākiya acattiṉ toṭarpu vēṟupaṭṭut tōṉṟum. ataṉāl akak karuviyākiya cintiyiṉkaṇ vēṟupaṭṭa ataṉ toṭarpu vantavāṟum, ataṉi nāmākavē mayaṅki avaṉāl piṇip puṇṭu kiṭakku māṟum, namakkum avaṟkum toṭar poṉṟum illātavāṟum viḷaṅkum. avviḷakkattāl āṉmāvākiya nammiyē attoṭar piṉiṉṟum nīkki niṟuttik kāṇat toṭaṅkuvōm. nammi aṟintālaṉṟi nām nammi attoṭarpiṉiṉṟum nīkka muṭiyātu, nammiyē aṟiya muyalvōmāyiṉ, aṟiya niṟkiṉṟa uṭal karuvi mutaliya acatti nikkuvatu iṉitu kikūṭum. ivvāṟē nammuṭiya muṉṉōr kaḷākiya cāṉṟōrkaḷ muyaṉṟirukkiṉṟārkaḷ. paṭṭiṉat taṭikaḷ.
“	mēviya puṉmayirt tokiyō, ammayir
 	pāviya tōliṉ parappō, tōliṭip
 	pukaviṭṭup potinta puṇṇō, puṇṇiṭi
 	ūṟum utirap puṉalō, kūṟuceytu
 	iṭiyiṭi niṟkum elumpō, elumpiṭi
 	muṭikeḻu mūḷi viḻutō, vaḻuvaḻuttu
 	uḷḷiṭi yoḻukum vaḻumpō, meḷḷaniṉṟu
 	ūrum puḻuviṉ oḻuṅkō, nīriṭi
 	vitta malattiṉ kuppiyō, vittuk
 	kaṭṭiya narampiṉ kayiṟō, uṭampiṟkuḷ
 	piriyā toṟukkum piṇiyō, teriyātu
 iṉṉatu yāṉeṉṟu aṟiyēṉ, eṉṉi
 eṅkum tēṭiṉaṉ, yātilum kāṇēṉ.........
 tiruviṭi maruta! poruviṭip pāka!
 maṅki paṅka! kaṅki nāyaka! niṉ
 teyvat tiruvaruḷ kivantu kiṭittaliṉ
 māyap paṭalam kīṟit tūya
 ñāṉa nāṭṭam peṟṟapiṉ yāṉum
 niṉperun taṉmiyum kaṇṭēṉ; kāṇṭalum
 eṉṉiyum kaṇṭēṉ, piṟariyum kaṇṭēṉ;
 niṉṉili yaṉittiyum kaṇṭēṉ; eṉṉē!
 niṉṉik kāṇā māntar
 taṉṉiyum kāṇāt taṉmiyōrē ”*
eṉṟum. “nāṉār eṉṉuḷḷamār ñāṉaṅkaḷār eṉṉi yāraṟivār, vāṉōrpirāṉ eṉṉi āṇṭilaṉēl” eṉṟu māṇikkavācakaṉārum ñāṉakkaṇ koṇṭu cintiyil nāṭiyatu kāṇalām.
ivvaṇṇam, uṭal karuvi mutaliya acatti vēṟu vittu ārāyumpōtu, kaṭuveyilil pēyttēr (kāṉal) taṇṇīr pōl kāṭci taruvatupōla, nammiṉ vēṟākātu oṉṟāy nāmākavē kāṭciyaḷitta acattup pēyttēr pōla nīṅki maṟintoḻiyum; oḻiyavē, iṟivaṉatu tiruvaruḷ niliyamākiya avaṉ tiruvaṭi nīḻal kāṭcippaṭum; āṉmāvum anta nīḻalic ceṉṟu cērntuviṭum. itaṉi āciriyar “urāttuṉit tērtteṉap pācam oruvat taṇṇiḻalām pati” eṉṟu aṟivittaruḷukiṉṟār, paṭṭiṉattaṭikaḷum,  “tūya ñāṉa nāṭṭam peṟṟapiṉ māyap paṭalam kīṟi niṉ perun taṉmiyum kaṇṭēṉ” eṉṟu kūṟuvatu kāṇka. pēyt tēri āciriyar urāttuṉit tēr eṉṟu iyampukiṉṟār. urā eṉpatu ūrā eṉpataṉ kuṟukkam. tuṉi, virivu, oruvarālum ūrappaṭātu virintu cellum tēr eṉṟu pēyttēr kuṟikkap paṭukiṟatu. kutiri maliyi ōṭāk kutiri eṉṟum, vēṅki marattip pāyā vēṅki eṉṟum cāṉṟōr col laḻaku tōṉṟak kūṟuvatu vaḻakkam. itaṉi veḷippaṭi yeṉṉum aṇivaki eṉpar. ivvāṟē pēyt tēri, urāttuṉittēr eṉṟu āciriyar aruḷic ceykiṉṟār. caṅkac cāṉṟōrum, “uruvil pēey ūrāt tēr” (akam. 67) eṉṟu colliyiruk kiṉṟārkaḷ.
ivvāṟu ñāṉanāṭṭam peṟṟu māyappaṭalam kīṟi mutalvaṉ tiruvaṭi nīḻalikkaṇṭa tirunāvukkaracar,
“	mācil vīṇiyum māli matiyamum
 	vīcu teṉṟalum vīṅkiḷa vēṉilum
 	mūcu vaṇṭaṟip poykiyum pōṉṟatē
 	īcaṉ enti iṇiyaṭi nīḻalē.”
eṉṟu tiruvāy malarntaruḷiyirukkiṉṟār.
iṉi, uṭaliṭamāka niṉṟu ñāṉakkaṇ koṇṭu cintiyil nāṭi, mutalvaṉ tiruvaruḷ niliyik kaṇṭa piṉpu, ataṉiṭamē oṉṟiyiruppataṟkē uḷḷattil viruppa muṇṭākum; tiruvaruḷ kāṭci tōṉṟum pōtē pēriṉpam curakkum; “cintipvarkkuc ciṟantu centēṉ muntip poḻivaṉa (mutalvaṉ) aṭittalam” eṉṟu tirunāvukkaracar aruḷuvatik kāṇalām. iṉpatti nāṭuvatē uyirkku iyalpātalāl, nam uḷḷam tiruvaruḷ kāṭciyiṉpattip peṟuvataṟkē peritum virumpum. āyiṉum, avviruppatti, uṭal karuvi ulakukaḷ pōntu citittu, viruppaminta uyiriṉ uḷḷattit tām kavarntu koḷḷum. pāci paṭarnta nīrniliyil kalliviṭṭeṟintāl, kal viḻuntaviṭattil appāci ciṟitu nīṅkum; piṉṉar vantu mūṭikkoḷḷum; atuvē pōl, tiruvaruṭkāṭciyil muyalumpōtu acattākiya pācattil toṭarpu nīṅkum akkāṭci nīṅkumpōtu ap pācam vantu mūṭiviṭukiṟatu. itaṉi,  “pācipaṭu kuṭṭattil kalliṉi viṭṭeṟiyappaṭum poḻu tu nīṅki atu viṭumpoḻutil parakkum; mācu paṭumalamāyi aruṅkaṉmam aṉittum araṉaṭiyi uṇarum pōtu akalum, piṉ naṇukum” eṉṟu aruṇanti civaṉār nam āciriyar pāl nērmukamākak kēṭṭaṟintu uṇarntu collu kiṉṟār araṉaṭiyi uṇarum uyir, anta aṭiyiṉ kīḻē irukka virumpum eṉpatu uṇmiyē; āṉāl avvāṟu iruppataṟku vaḻi yātu? ataṟku oru vaḻi uṇṭu; aḵtāvatu tiruvin teḻutti iṭiyaṟāmal muṟippaṭi eṇṇuvatu eṉpārāy, “viti eṇṇum añceḻuttē” eṉṟu āciriyar aṟivuṟuttaruḷu
kiṉṟār. eṇṇum - eṇṇa vēṇṭum; nīr itic collum eṉṟāl nīr itic colla vēṇṭum eṉṟu poruḷāvatupōla eṇṇum eṉpatu eṇṇa vēṇṭum eṉṟu poruḷpaṭukiṟatu.
iṉi, tiruvin teḻutteṉpatu, ci, va, ya, na, ma eṉṟa ainteḻuttukkaḷiyum kuṟikkum. ivvinteḻuttiyum kūṭṭavarum civaya nama eṉpatu vaṭamoḻiyil oru coṟ ṟoṭarākum. iṅkē, “atu coṟṟoṭar aṉṟu; eḻuttu,” eṉpataṟkākavē tiruvinteḻuttu eṉṟu ciṟappāka eṭuttuk kūṟappaṭukiṟatu. ivveḻuttukkaḷ iṟivaṉ tiruppeyarkaḷuḷ oṉṟāṉa namaciva eṉpatil uḷḷa eḻuttukkaḷ. namaci vāya eṉpatu iṟivaṉ tiruppeyar. ati māṟṟic civāyanama eṉṟāl peyarttaṉmi nīṅki eḻuttākiviṭukiṟatu tiruvin teḻutti niṉintu ōtavēṇṭumāyiṉ, namacivāya eṉṟa tiruppeyari māṟṟi eḻuttākkuvatu mutal viti; illiyāṉāl tiruvin teḻuttukkaḷi ilakkaṇappaṭi peṟa muṭiyātu. “nanti nāmam namaccivāya” eṉṟu tiruñāṉa campantarum, “entiyār tirunāmam namaccivāya” eṉṟu tirunāvukkaracarum aruḷic ceytiruppatu kāṇka.
ivveḻuttukkaḷil ci eṉpatu civaparamporuḷiyum, va eṉpatu avaṉatu aruḷiyum, ya eṉpatu uyiriyum, na eṉpatu uyir acattōṭu kūṭum pōtu catti niṉiyātu acattāy iyintu acattu nalkum nukarccikaḷi nukarvataṟkum, acatti viṭṭuc cattākiya civattōṭu kūṭumpōtu acatti niṉiyātu civattōṭu civamāy iyintu civapōkat ti nukarvataṟkum uyiri maṟittu niṉṟu iyakkum iṟivaṉu ṭiya maṟippuc cattiyiyum, ma eṉpatu malattiyum kuṟik kum. ivveḻut tintiṉiyum eṇṇuṅkāl, civattukkum uyirkaḷukkum uḷḷa toṭarpum, civattukkum ulakattukku muḷḷa toṭarpum, uyirkaḷukkum ulakattukkumuḷḷa toṭarpum, attoṭar puṇṭāṉa kāraṇamum, ataṉāl viḷiyum payaṉum eṇṇattukku vantu iṟivaṉ tiruvaruḷ nīḻali viṭṭu nīṅkātu niṟkum nilimiyik kivarac ceyyum. itaṉi,
“	aṇṇal mutalā aḻakār eḻuttintum
 	eṇṇil irāppakalaṟ ṟiṉpattē-naṇṇi
 	aruḷā ṉatucivattē yākkum aṇuvi
 	iruḷā ṉatutīra iṉṟu*”
eṉṟu tiruvatiki-maṉavācaṅ kaṭantār kūṟiyaruḷuvatu kāṇalām. ivvāṟu ittiruvinteḻuttiyē puṇiyākap paṟṟik koṇṭu iṭiyaṟātu eṇṇip pēriṉpam curakkap peṟṟu nukarnta maṇivācakap perumāṉ.
“	nāṉēyō tavam ceytēṉ! civayanama eṉappeṟṟēṉ.
 	tēṉāy iṉ ṉamutamumā māyt tittikkum civaperumāṉ
 	tāṉēvantu eṉatuḷḷam pukuntuaṭiyēṟ karuḷceytāṉ
 	ūṉāru muyirvāḻkki yoṟuttaṉṟē veṟuttiṭavē.”
eṉṟu tēṉcoṭṭap pāṭum tiruvācakattāl nām aṟiya aruḷic ceykiṉṟār.
10. ivvāṟu tiruvinteḻutti vitippaṭi yeṇṇu vatāl iṟivaṉukkum ulakukkum ulakil vāḻum uyirkaḷākiya namakkum uḷḷa toṭarpu viḷaṅkum eṉṟu muṉpu kaṇṭōm. nuṇṇiya māyiyākiya uḷporuḷiliruntu uṭal karuvi ulaku mutaliyavaṟṟi iṟivaṉ paṭikkiṟāṉ. ataṉāl avaṉ ceypavaṉākiṉṟāṉ; uṭal karuviyulakukaḷ ceyyappaṭum poruḷkaḷākiṉṟaṉa. ceyyappaṭum poruḷ ceypavaṉatu uṭimiyākum. evvāṟeṉiṉ, māyi eṉpatu mutalvaṉuṭiya tiruvaruḷāṟṟali (cattiyi) iṭamākak koṇṭu kiṭakkum oruvaki āṟṟal; anta āṟṟalikkalakki uṭal karuvi ulakukaḷic ceytaruḷuvatu paṟṟi avaṉ ceypavaṉāṉāṉ; avi ceyappaṭu poruḷkaḷāyiṉa. taṉpāl uḷḷa oṉṟikkoṇṭu oru cey poruḷi oruvaṉ ceyvāṉāyiṉ acceyappaṭuporuḷ avaṉatu uṭimiyākiṟa taṉṟō? atupōlavē ivvulakamum ulakiyaṟ poruḷum avaṟṟiṭi nilavum nam uṭalum ataṟkuḷ irukkum karuvikaḷum ākiyayāvum iṟivaṉuṭiya uṭimi yākum. itupaṟṟiyē maṇivācakar mutaliya aruṭ celvar iṟivaṉi, “uṭiyavaṉē” eṉṟum, “uṭiyāy” eṉṟum aṭikkaṭi eṭuttōtiyirukkiṉṟaṉar. ivvuṭal karuvi mutaliyaṉa iṟivaṉatu uṭimiyēyāyiṉum ivaṟṟip payaṉkoḷḷuvatu uyirākiya āṉmāvēyaṉṟi avaṉaṉṟu. āṉmā ivvuṭal karuvi mutaliyavaṟṟit taṉṉuṭiya poruḷākavē eṇṇi avaṟṟiṉ pāl paṟṟukkoḷḷukiṟatu. tanti oruvaṉ oru caṭṭiyit tittuk koṇṭuvantu taṉ makaṉukkuk koṭuttāl, avaṉ ataṉit taṉatākak koṇṭu ataṉpāl paṟṟukkoḷḷukiṟāṉ; atupōlavē, āṉmāviṉ ceykiyum uḷatu. tāṉ paṟṟu vittup payaṉ koḷḷum caṭṭiyi utaviya tantiyi, makaṉ “entiyē” eṉa aṉpu ceyyum muṟimiyilēyē, nam cāṉṟōr civaperumāṉāṉa mutalvaṉit tantiyē eṉṟum, entiyē eṉṟum, palapaṭiyāka aṉpu moḻikaḷāl aḻippati avarkaḷiṉ tirumuṟikaḷil pārkkiṉṟōm.
iṉi, ‘iṟivaṉ ivaṟṟi uṇṭupaṇṇi āṉmākkaḷukkuk koṭuppāṉēṉ’ eṉṟōr eṇṇam inniliyil namakku uṇṭākiṟatu. āṉmākkaḷi anātiyē malamāṉatu piṇittu maṟittu iruḷil kiṭattiyirukkiṟatu; taṉṉip pōlavē aṟivu viḻivu ceyal mutaliya āṟṟal paṭitta cattākiya āṉmā malattāl kaṭṭuṇṭirukkum niliyi nōkkiyaruḷum iṟivaṉ, āṉmāviṉpāl pēraruḷ koṇṭu māyiyiliruntu ivvuṭal karuvi mutaliyavaṟṟip paṭittuk koṭuttu utavukiṉṟāṉ. āṟṟal vaṭivākiya māyi eṉpatu oḷi mayamāka irukkiṟateṉṟu iṉṟiya viññāṉa aṟivu uṟutippaṭuttukitu. malaviruḷil kiṭakkum uyir āṟṟalākiya oḷipporuḷiliruntu uṇṭākkappaṭum uṭal karuvi mutaliyavaṟṟit tuṇiyākak koṇṭu, aṟivatum, viḻivatum, ceyvatum ceytu malaviruḷiliruntu viṭutalipeṟa muyalkiṟatu.  ivvakiyil mutalvaṉ taṉporuṭṭu uṭal karuvi mutaliya vaṟṟip paṭittu malaviruḷiliruntu vīṭupēṟu eytuvataṟ kākat taṉakku nalkiyiruppati uyir niṉikkum; niṉinta vaḻi, ataṟku am mutalvaṉpāl aḷaviṟanta naṉṟiyuṇarvu uṇṭākum. ataṉāl ovvoru kaṇamum iṟivaṉukku naṉṟi celuttum kaṭami uyirkku mikukiṉṟatu. 
ivvaṇṇam uṭal karuvikaḷi nalkiya iṟivaṉ, am maṭṭil niṉṟu pōkātu, avaṟṟōṭu kalantu oṉṟāyum, uyiroṭu kūṭi uṭaṉāyum uṇṇiṉṟu tōṉṟāttuṇi ceyvatu tiruvin teḻutti vitip paṭi ōtuvatāl uṇarappaṭukiṟatu; uṭalōṭu kūṭi uyir ceyyum ovvoru ceyal nikaḻccikkum iṟivaṉ tōṉṟā mutalāy iruppatum viḷaṅkukiṟatu. avaṉatu kalappilliyāyiṉ uṭal karuvi mutaliyavaṟṟil oḻuṅkāṉa ceyal nikaḻātu; malattāl maṟip puṇṭiruttalāl uyirkkum aṟivu mutaliyaṉa toḻiṟ paṭā; ‘avaṉaṉṟi ōraṇuvum aciyātu’ eṉpatu uṇmiyākiṟatu.
ivvāṟu ellāc ceyaṟkum iṟivaṉē muḻumutalāka iruppavum, avaṉ uyiri muṉṉiruttit tāṉ ataṉōṭu oṉṟiyuṭaṉāy niṟkiṉṟāṉ. ataṉāl, uyir, etiyum tāṉē ceyvatākavum tāṉē muḻu mutalākavum niṉintum colliyum ceytum oḻukukiṟatu. itaṉāl viḷiyum naṉmi tīmikaṭku uyirē poṟuppāḷiyākiṉṟatu. appoṟuppukku āṉmākkaḷākiya nām uriyarākā viṭṭāl viḷiyum naṉmi tīmikaḷ  nammic cēra māṭṭā. itaṟku vaḻi yātu?
ituvariyil nammi muṟpaṭa vittu nammuḷ kalantu uṭaṉāy niṉṟa mutalvaṉi muṉṉē niṟutti avaṉukkuḷ nammi oṉṟuvippatutāṉ itaṟkut takka vaḻiyām. iti namatu āciriyar, “avaṉē tāṉē ākiya anneṟi, ēkaṉāki iṟipaṇi niṟka” eṉṟu aṟivuṟut taruḷukiṉṟār. avaṉ eṉṟatu iṟivaṉiyum, tāṉ eṉṟatu āṉmāviyum kuṟikkum, kuṟikkavē, iṟivaṉ uyirkaḷiṉ uṭal kalantu uḷaṅkalantu uyirēyāy oṉṟiyirukkum anneṟiyē mēṟ koṇṭu ‘uṭal karuvikaḷiṉ ākkam eṉataṉṟu, ātalāl avaṟṟiṉ ceyalvakikkaṭkum iyakkattukkum mutalvaṉyāṉ allaṉ, ellāvaṟṟikkum muḻumutal iṟivaṉē’ eṉak kaḻittu avaṉpāl oṉṟiviṭa vēṇṭum eṉpatu viḷaṅkum. appōtu uṭal karuvikaḷiṉ iyakkattāl uṇṭākum paṇikaṭku iṟivaṉ poṟuppāḷiyākiṉṟāṉ. ellām iṟivaṉ paṇiyāki viṭutalāl, ēkaṉāki niṟka nikaḻum ceyal iṟipaṇi yeṉappaṭuva tāyiṟṟu. ‘ivvāṟu vāḻvatutāṉ vāḻveṉappaṭum; vēṟupaṭa vāḻvatu ‘vāḻvākātu’ eṉpatu tōṉṟavē, maṇivācakap perumāṉ, “vāḻkiṉṟāy vāḻāta neñcamē valviṉip
paṭṭu, āḻkiṉṟāy” eṉṟum, vāḻa vēṇṭiya vāḻvāvatu, “eṇṇamē yuṭal vāymūkkoṭu cevi kaṇ eṉṟivi niṉkaṇṇē vittu” vāḻvatu eṉṟum eṭutturittuḷḷār.
aṉṟiyum, iṟivaṉi  muṟpaṭa niṟutti ēkaṉāki iṟi paṇiyil niṉṟa tiṟatti, “kūṟum nāvē mutalākak kūṟum karaṇam ellām nī, tēṟum vaki nī, tikippum nī, tīmi naṉmi muḻutum nī, vēṟōr paricu iṅku oṉṟillēṉ” eṉṟum, “vēṇṭi nī yātu aruḷ ceytāyyāṉum atuvē vēṇṭiṉallāl, vēṇṭum paricu oṉṟu uṇṭeṉṉil atuvum uṉṟaṉ viruppaṉṟē” eṉṟum. “naṉṟē ceyvāy piḻi ceyvāy nāṉō itaṟku nāyakamē” eṉṟum varum tiru vācaṅkaḷ viḷakkukiṉṟaṉa.
ivvāṟu nāṉ eṉṟum eṉatu eṉṟum eḻum eṇṇaṅ kaṭku iṭaṅkoṭātu keṭuttu ēkaṉāki niṟpatāl, appōtu nikaḻum ceyalkaḷi mutalvaṉ tāṉē eṉṟu koḷvaṉ; itaṉi āciriyarkku muṉṉē vāḻnta cāṉṟōrkaḷum, “nam ceyal aṟṟu inta nām aṟṟapiṉ nātaṉ, taṉ ceyaltāṉē eṉṟu untīpaṟa” eṉṟu terivit tuḷḷārkaḷ.
malakkaṭṭāl iruḷiṟkiṭanta namporuṭṭu uṭal karuvi ulakukaḷākiya māyiyiṉ toṭarpu namakku eytu vikkap paṭṭa taṉṟō! attoṭarpu uṇṭāṉatum viṉikaḷ tōṉṟiṉa;  avaṟṟiṟku nāmē muḻumutal eṉa niṉintu ceytamiyāl, avaṟṟiṉ payaṉi nukartaṟ poruṭṭu nām piṟappiṟappukkaṭku uḷḷāki uḻaṉṟōm. ēkaṉāki iṟi paṇi niṟkaluṟṟāl viṉit toṭarpu keṭukiṟatu; atu keṭavē māyiyiṉ toṭarpu keṭukiṟatu; avaṟṟiṉkaṇ nikaḻum ceyalkaḷ iṟivaṉ ceyal kaḷākiṉṟaṉa. avaṉ ceyal muṟṟum āṉmākkaḷākiya nām malattiṉ maṟip piṉiṉṟum nīṅki viṭutali peṟuvatu kuṟittu nikaḻvataṉāl malamaṟippum nīṅkukiṟatu. itupaṟṟiyē āciriyar, “ēkaṉāki iṟipaṇi niṟka, malamāyi taṉṉoṭu valviṉi yiṉṟē” eṉṟu aruḷiyuḷḷār. ikkaruttukkaḷiyē,
“	avaṉē tāṉē yākiya anneṟi
 	ēkaṉāki iṟipaṇi niṟka
 	malamāyi taṉṉoṭu valviṉi iṉṟē”
eṉṟu āciriyar urittaruḷikiṉṟār.
11. uṭal karuvi mutaliyavaṟṟōṭu kūṭi avaṟṟit taṉ uṭimiyeṉṟum, avaṟṟāl nikaḻum ceyalkaṭkut tāṉē mutal eṉṟum karuti yoḻukivanta uyir, nāṉ, eṉatu eṉṟa niṉivum ceyalum aṟṟiruppatu arumiyātalāl, ataṟku vaḻiyātu? eṉṟōr aiyam namakkuṇṭāvatu iyalpu. an niṉivuc ceyalkaḷi muḻumutalāka niṟuttiya iṟivaṉpāl aṉpu celuttuvatil celaviṭavēṇṭum; ituvē ataṟku vaḻi eṉṟu āciriyar aṟivuṟuttat talip paṭukiṉṟār.
namakku oruvarpāl aṉpuṇṭāka vēṇṭumāyiṉ, avarukkum namakkum evvakiyilēṉum toṭarpu uṇṭākavēṇṭum. oruvakiyilum toṭarpillāta variṭattil namakku aṉpuṇṭāva tilli. aṉṟiyum, toṭarpuṭiyāruṭiya toṭarpi aṟiyātiruntālum maṟantālum namakku avariṭattil aṉpu tōṉṟuvatilli. itu  nāṭōṟum nikaḻum nikaḻcci. immuṟiyē nām iṟivaṉ pāl aṉpu ceyyavēṇṭu māyiṉ namakkum avaṉukkum uḷḷa toṭarpik kāṇavēṇṭum. tiruvin teḻutti vitippaṭi eṇṇuvatāl namakkum iṟivaṉukkum uḷḷa toṭarpu viḷakkamākiṟatu. nām malattāl kaṭṭuṇṭu malaviruḷil aṟivu ceyalaṟṟukkiṭantatum, iṟivaṉ nampāl aruḷkūrntu māyiyiliruntu uṭal karuvi mutaliya vaṟṟi ākkit tantatum, aṟivillātaviyākiya av vuṭal mutaliyaṉa tattamakkuriya ceyalkaḷil oḻuṅku keṭātu cenneṟi piṟaḻātu iyalutaṟ poruṭṭu avaṟṟōṭu oṉṟiyum avvuṭaliṭattē karuvikaḷōṭu oṉṟiyirukkum aṟivuṭip poruḷākiya uyiriṉ aṟivu ceyalkaḷ eḻuntu toḻiṟpaṭutaṟ poruṭṭu uṭaṉā kiyum iruppatum naṉku viḷaṅkukiṉṟaṉa. ivvakiyil iṟivaṉ namakkuc ceyyum utavi teḷivāka viḷaṅka viḷaṅka, namakku avaṉpāl uḷḷa toṭarpuṇarvum attoṭarpāl aṉpum uṇṭākiṉṟaṉa. avaṉ ceyta utavikaḷilum nammōṭu oṉṟi uṭaṉāy niṉṟu ceyyum utaviyi niṉikkum pōtu anta aṉpu elliyaṟṟatāy virikiṟatu.
iṟivaṉ nammōṭu oṉṟiyuṭaṉāy niṉṟu ceyyum utaviyātu? nām kaṇṇāl oru poruḷik kāṇpatāka vittuk koḷvōm. appōtu nam aṟivu, kaṇṇoḷi vēṟu, tāṉ vēṟu eṉa vēṟṟumiyillāmal ataṉōṭu oṉṟi uṭaṉāy niṉṟu ap poruḷik kaṇṇik kāṇacceykiṟatu. appōtu nām apporuḷik kaṇṭōm eṉṟum, nam kaṇkaṇṭateṉṟum kūṟukiṟōm. ivviṭattē nam uyiraṟivu kaṇṇoḷiyoṭu kalantu uṭaṉāy niṉṟu mutalil kaṇṇik kāṇac ceyvatum piṉṉart tāṉ kāṇpatumākiya iruceyalkaḷ nikaḻvatu terikiṟatu. ivvāṟē iṟivaṉum āṉmāvōṭu oṉṟi uṭaṉāy niṉṟu poruḷkaḷi āṉmāvik kāṇac ceyvatum piṉpu tāṉ kāṇpatum ākiya iru pēru tavikaḷic ceytaruḷukiṉṟāṉ. itaṉiyē nam āciriyar, “kāṇum kaṇṇukkuk kāṭṭum uḷam pōla, kāṇa uḷḷattik kāṭṭik” kāṇkiṉṟāṉ eṉṟu urit taruḷu kiṉṟār. kaṇṭu kāṭṭukiṉṟāṉ eṉṟu cūttiram kūṟiṉum, kāṭṭik kāṇkiṉṟāṉ eṉa māṟṟik koḷḷa vēṇṭum.
mēlum, nām oṉṟik kāṇumpōtu nammuṭiya āṉma aṟivum kaṇṇum ceyyum ceyalkaḷ pirittaṟiya vārātapaṭi oṉṟi uṭaṉ nikaḻkiṉṟaṉa; avvaṇṇamē iṟivaṉ ceyalum āṉma aṟivum pirittaṟiya vārātapaṭi oṉṟi uṭaṉ nikaḻkiṉṟaṉa. ivvāṟu iṟivaṉ namporuṭṭuk kāṭṭu vatum kāṇpatumākiya iruvakip pērutavikaḷic ceyvati niṉikkum pōtu avaṉpāl vaṟṟāta pēraṉpu perukukiṟatu. aṉpiṉ viḷivu aruḷ. aṉpu perukuṅkāl aruḷē uruvākavuḷḷa iṟivaṉiṭattē atu nammic celuttiviṭukiṟatu. āṉmā avaṉatu tiruvaṭiyākiya aruḷ niliyattic cērntu pēriṉpattip peṟukiṟatu. itaṉit tāṉ nam āciriyar.
“	kāṇum kaṇṇukkuk kāṭṭum uḷampōl
 	kāṇa vuḷḷattik kaṇṭu kāṭṭaliṉ
 	ayarā aṉpiṉ araṉkaḻal celumē.” 
eṉṟu urittaruḷukiṉṟār, uḷḷattik kāṭṭik kāṇṭaliṉ ayarā aṉpum, ayarā aṉpiṉāl araṉ kaḻalip peṟutalum payaṉatalāl. “kaṇṭu kāṭṭaliṉ ayarā aṉpiṉ araṇkaḻal celumē” eṉṟu aruḷi yuḷḷār.
12. mēlē coṉṉapaṭi iṟivaṉōṭu ēkaṉāki iṟi paṇi niṟpatum, avaṉ āṉmāvāviṉ poruṭṭuk kāṭṭum utavi, kāṇum utavi eṉṟa iruvaki utavikaḷiccey toḻukuvati niṉintu avaṉpāl ayarāta aṉpu ceyvatum eṉṟa iruvaki aṟaṅkaḷum cittānta civac cenneṟi eṉṟum; tiruneṟi eṉṟum civaneṟi eṉṟum vaḻaṅkum. ivaṟṟi mēṟkoṇṭu oḻukupavaṉ civañāṉi yāvaṉ. āyiṉum avaṉ māyiyiliruntu uṇṭākiya ivvuṭal karuvikaḷōṭu kūṭi ulakiyaṟ poruḷkaḷip peṟṟu ulaka vāḻvu vāḻavēṇṭiyavaṉāka irukkiṉṟāṉ. civañāṉam peṟutaṟku muṉṉē, avaṉi iṟukap paṟṟi niṉṟa malam iṟivaṉuṭiya naṟṟāḷic cērātapaṭi maṟittuk koṇṭiruntatu ippōtum atu nīṅkavilli. ammalattiṉ maṟippu nekiḻntu nīṅkum poruṭṭu vantu kūṭiya uṭal karuvi mutaliya māyā kāriyaṅkaḷ vantu iyakkiyapōtu avaṟṟiṉ vaḻiyē niṉṟu avaṟṟiyē tāṉeṉa eṇṇi mayaṅkip pōṉāṉ. māyiyōṭu kūṭi niṟkiyil nikaḻum iyakkam viṉit toṭarpāy niṉṟatu. civañāṉam peṟṟa piṉ, avi vēṟu tāṉ vēṟu eṉa eṇṇi, muṉṉarccoṉṉa iruvakic cenneṟikaḷiyum mēṟkoṇṭu ulakiyal vāḻvu naṭattumpōtum, ‘anta malamāyi viṉikaḷiṉ toṭarpil piṇippuṇṭu kiṭantu paḻakiya paḻakkam avaṉi viṭātaṉṟō? atu vantu tākkā tiruppataṟku ulakiyal vāḻvu araṇākātē; itaṟku eṉceyvatu?’ eṉpaṉa pōlum aiyaṅkaḷ iṅkē eḻukiṉṟaṉa. avaṟṟiṟku viḷakkam tarappaṭukiṉṟaṉa.
tiruneṟi mēṟkoṇṭeḻukum civañāṉiyāl malam iruntapōtilum. maṟippuṇṭu aṟivu aṟi pōkā vaṇṇam taṉṉip pātukāttukkoḷḷal avaṉ kaṭamiyākum. ataṟkākavē, āciriyar, “cemmalar nōṉṟāḷ cēra lōṭṭā ammala”ttik kaḻuvikkoḷka eṉṟu aṟivuṟuttukiṉṟār. iṟivaṉ tiruvaṭi centāmari pōṉṟa ciṟappuṭiyateṉpatu viḷakkac “cemmalart tāḷ” eṉṟār. civañāṉac celvarkaḷiṉ ceṉṉiyilum cintiyilum malarntu viḷaṅkum cemmiyuṭiya tāḷā talāl ivvāṟu atu ciṟappikkappaṭukiṟatu. malar pōla meṉmiyuṭiya tāyiṉum taṉpāl vantu uṟṟa āṉmāk kaḷi mīḷappirintu ēkātavāṟu āṭkoṇṭaruḷum vaṉmiyuṭiyatu iṟivaṉ cemmalart tiruvaṭi. ataṉāl ataṉi “nōṉṟāḷ” eṉṟu ciṟappikkiṉṟār. nōṉmi, vaṉmi; “uṟunart tāṅkiya mataṉuṭi nōṉṟāḷ” eṉṟu nakkiraṉārum kūṟuvatu kāṇka. “cēra loṭṭā am malam” eṉṟatu. niṉiya viṭātu uyiraṟivi maṟittup piṇittuk koṇṭirunta anta āṇava malam eṉṟu poruḷpaṭukiṟatu. iyalpāṉa aṟivāṟṟalāl virinta paṇpu koṇṭu civamān taṉmi eytum ciṟappuṭiya āṉ māvip piṇittu iṟukki aṇup pōl ciṟumiyaṭiyac ceyvatu paṟṟi malatti āṇavamalam eṉṟu civākamaṅkaḷ peyar kūṟukiṉṟaṉa. civañāṉipāl nekiḻntu nīṅkātu kiṭakkum malam, civañāṉam peṟutaṟkumuṉ, nekiḻum nīrmiyiṉṟit tiṇṇitāyp piṇittirunta ciṟapput tōṉṟa “ammalam” eṉṟu kuṟittaruḷiṉār. malam pōntu maṟikkātavāṟu kāttal vēṇṭum eṉpataṟku “malam kaḻuvuka” eṉṟār. malatti nīkkutaṟku, ēṟṟa col kaḻuvutal eṉpatu. malattāl aṟivu aṟi pōkātapaṭi kāttuk koḷḷutaṟku vaḻi tiruvin teḻutti vitippaṭi eṇṇuvatēyākum. civañāṉikaḷ eppōtum it tiruvinteḻutti eṇṇuvatum ōtuvatum iyalpu; “nāṉ maṟakkiṉum collum nā namaccivāyavē” eṉṟum, “ainteḻuttiṉ puṇipiṭittuk kiṭakkiṉṟēṉ” eṉṟum, “viṉip pakikku attiramāvaṉa añceḻuttumē” eṉṟum. camayakuravarkaḷ kūṟuvatum, mikap paḻiyōrāṉa tirumūlanāyaṉār. “maraṇam ki vittu uyir māṟṟiṭum pōtu araṇam kikkūṭṭuvatu añceḻut tāmē” eṉavum, “aruḷvaḻi kāṭṭuvatu añ ceḻuttāmē” eṉavum ōtuvatum pōtiya cāṉṟukaḷām.
ivvāṟu tiruvinteḻutti iṭiyaṟātu niṉin tōti malam kaḻuvum civañāṉi ulakiyal vāḻvil palatiṟa makkaḷōṭu kūṭi vāḻavēṇṭiyavaṉ. avarkaḷ palarum civa ñāṉam peṟṟa varkaḷāka iruppar eṉpatu aritu. avarkaḷōṭum civañāṉi ottu vāḻa vēṇṭum.avvāṟu vāḻuṅkāl, avaratu kūṭṭattāl civañāṉam peṟṟārkkum viḻivum, veṟuppum, tōṉṟit tuṉpam ceyyumaṉṟō? itaṟkeṉa ceyvatu? eṉṟoru viṉā eḻukiṟatu. iḵtu uṇmiyē, nilattiyalpāl nīr tirivatupōla ettiṟat tōrkkum iṉattiyalpāl aṟivu tiriyum; itupaṟṟiyē periyōrkaḷ nalliṉa mallāta ciṟṟiṉattōri añci nīṅku kiṉṟaṉar. ātalāl, civañāṉac celvarkaḷ civaparam poruḷiṭattē aṉpuṭiyavarkaḷ evarkaḷō avarkaḷōṭē kūṭi vāḻvatu
tāṉ ceyyattakkatu; atutāṉ itaṟku vaḻi, itu paṟṟiyē 
“aṉpa roṭu maruvuka” eṉṟu āciriyar  aṟivikkiṉṟār. civaṉpāl aṉpuṭiya aṉpari evvāṟu aṟivatu?anta aṉpu avaravar cintiyilēyaṉṟō niṟpatu! āyiṉum, civattiṉpāl uṇmi yaṉpuṭiyār ellā vuyiriṭattum aṉpuṭiya
